# HyperWorks Desktop Tcl reference — HyperView Tcl Modify (Help 2022.3)

845 pages from the shared HWD reference route. This is a page-type grouping, not proof that every row is a callable API. Open the Altair page for its full contract. [Back to route index](./HWD_TCL_REFERENCE_CATALOG_2022_3.md).

| Official page | Exact Type / Application | Syntax | Purpose | Key inputs / outputs / returns |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| [ActivateView](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poibestview_activateview.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `bestview_handle ActivateView` | This command activates or updates the view with the current settings. | Not stated |
| [ClearHiddenSet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poibestview_clearhiddenset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `bestview_handle ClearHiddenSet` | This command clears the entry on SetHiddenSet. | Not stated |
| [hwi OpenStack](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwi_openstack.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `hwi OpenStack` | This command begins automatic handle tracking.Note: An hwi instance is always available.Handle stacks can be nested. | Not stated |
| [hwI3DViewCtrl ArcBall](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwi3dviewctrl_arcball.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `viewControl_handle ArcBall` | This command allows you to rotate the model by manipulating a virtual sphere. When the command is first executed, it waits for the cursor to move into the graphics area. The left mouse button can then be pressed and moved to rotate the model, the middle mouse button can be used to change the center of rotation by clicking on the model, and the right mouse button can be used to cancel the operation Note: Moving the mouse outside of the graphics area will also cancel the operation. | Not stated |
| [hwI3DViewCtrl CenterView](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwi3dviewctrl_centerview.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `viewControl_handle CenterView` | This command allows you to move the selected point to the center of the graphics area. When the command is first executed, it waits for the cursor to move into the graphics area. The left mouse button can then be pressed to select a point to move to the center, and the right mouse button can be used to cancel the operation Note: Moving the mouse outside of the graphics area will also cancel the operation. | Not stated |
| [hwI3DViewCtrl CircleZoom](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwi3dviewctrl_circlezoom.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `viewControl_handle CircleZoom` | This command allows you to zoom into the model by drawing a window around a particular region. When the command is first executed, it waits for the cursor to move into the graphics area. The left mouse button can then be pressed and moved to draw a window around the region to zoom in on. Moving the mouse outside of the graphics area cancels the operation. | Not stated |
| [hwI3DViewCtrl PanView](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwi3dviewctrl_panview.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `viewControl_handle PanView` | This command allows you to perform a dynamic pan of the model. When the command is first executed, it waits for the cursor to move into the graphics area. The left mouse button can then be held and dragged to pan, and the right mouse button can be used to cancel the operation.Note: Moving the mouse outside of the graphics area will also cancel the operation. | Not stated |
| [hwI3DViewCtrl RotateClockwise](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwi3dviewctrl_rotateclockwise.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `viewControl_handle RotateClockwise ?angle?` | This command allows you to rotate the model clockwise about an axis oriented perpendicular to the screen. The rotation angle, in degrees, can be specified. If it is not specified, the default rotation angle set using the SetRotationAngle command is used. | **Inputs:** angle The rotation angle, in degrees. |
| [hwI3DViewCtrl RotateCounterClockwise](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwi3dviewctrl_rotatecounterclockwise.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `viewControl_handle RotateCounterClockwise` | This command allows you to rotate the model counter-clockwise about an axis oriented perpendicular to the screen. The rotation angle is defined using the SetRotationAngle command. | Not stated |
| [hwI3DViewCtrl RotateDown](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwi3dviewctrl_rotatedown.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `viewControl_handle RotateDown ?angle?` | This command allows you to rotate the model down about an axis oriented horizontally across the screen. The rotation angle, in degrees, can be specified. If it is not specified, the default rotation angle set using the SetRotationAngle command is used. | **Inputs:** angle The rotation angle, in degrees. |
| [hwI3DViewCtrl RotateLeft](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwi3dviewctrl_rotateleft.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `viewControl_handle RotateLeft ?angle?` | This command allows you to rotate the model left about an axis oriented vertically across the screen. The rotation angle, in degrees, can be specified. If it is not specified, the default rotation angle set using the SetRotationAngle command is used. | **Inputs:** angle The rotation angle, in degrees. |
| [hwI3DViewCtrl RotateRight](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwi3dviewctrl_rotateright.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `viewControl_handle RotateRight ?angle?` | This command allows you to rotate the model right about an axis oriented vertically across the screen. The rotation angle, in degrees, can be specified. If it is not specified, the default rotation angle set using the SetRotationAngle command is used. | **Inputs:** angle The rotation angle, in degrees. |
| [hwI3DViewCtrl RotateUp](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwi3dviewctrl_rotateup.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `viewControl_handle RotateUp ?angle?` | This command allows you to rotate the model up about an axis oriented horizontally across the screen. The rotation angle, in degrees, can be specified. If it is not specified, the default rotation angle set using the SetRotationAngle command is used. | **Inputs:** angle The rotation angle, in degrees. |
| [hwI3DViewCtrl ScaleView](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwi3dviewctrl_scaleview.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `viewControl_handle ScaleView` | This command allows you to perform a dynamic zoom of the model. When the command is first executed, it waits for the cursor to move into the graphics area. The left mouse button can then be held and dragged in/out to zoom, and the right mouse button can be used to cancel the operation.Note: Moving the mouse outside of the graphics area will also cancel the operation. | Not stated |
| [hwI3DViewCtrl TumbleView](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwi3dviewctrl_tumbleview.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `viewControl_handle TumbleView` | This command allows you to perform a dynamic rotation of the model. When the command is first executed, it waits for the cursor to move into the graphics area. The left mouse button can then be pressed and moved to rotate the model, the middle mouse button can be used to change the center of rotation by clicking on the model, and the right mouse button can be used to cancel the operation.Note: Moving the mouse outside of the graphics area will also cancel the operation. | Not stated |
| [hwIAMFExportOptions SetEncoder](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwiamfexportoptions_setencoder.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `hwIAMFExportOptions_handle SetEncoder encoder` | This command sets the type of encoding used when exporting AMF video files.Note: This parameter is used when exporting AMF files from any client during video capture. | **Inputs:** encoder The encoder type. Options are: “jpeg” or “zlib”. |
| [hwIAMFExportOptions SetPalette](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwiamfexportoptions_setpalette.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `hwIAMFExportOptions_handle SetPalette type` | This command sets the palette used when exporting AMF files.Note: This parameter is used when exporting AMF files from either the video or animation clients during video capture. | **Inputs:** type The palette type. Options include: "fullcolor" or "grayscale". |
| [hwIAMFExportOptions SetQuality](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwiamfexportoptions_setquality.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `hwIAMFExportOptions_handle SetQuality quality` | This command sets the quality used when exporting AMF files using the JPEG encoder.Note: This parameter is used when exporting JPEG encoded AMF files from any client during video capture. | **Inputs:** quality The quality to be used when exporting JPEG encoded AMF files. This must be between 0 and 100 (exclusive). |
| [hwIAMFExportOptions SetSize](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwiamfexportoptions_setsize.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `hwIAMFExportOptions_handle SetSize size` | This command sets the relative image size (percent) used when exporting AMF files.Note: This parameter is used when exporting AMF files from either the video or animation clients during video capture. | **Inputs:** size The relative image size (in percent) to be used when exporting AMF files. Values include: 100, 75, 50, or 33. |
| [hwIAnimator Next](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwianimator_next.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `hwIAnimator_handle Next` | This command advances the animator to the next frame. If the animator is at the last frame, Next wraps to the first frame. | Not stated |
| [hwIAnimator Previous](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwianimator_previous.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `hwIAnimator_handle Previous` | This command advances the animator to the previous frame. If the animator is at the first frame, Previous wraps to the last frame. | Not stated |
| [hwIAnimator SetBounce](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwianimator_setbounce.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `hwIAnimator_handle SetBounce bounce` | This command sets the current animator's bounce state. | **Inputs:** bounce The bounce state. True False |
| [hwIAnimator SetCurrentFrame](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwianimator_setcurrentframe.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `hwIAnimator_handle SetCurrentFrame index` | This command sets the current frame index. | **Inputs:** index The frame index. |
| [hwIAnimator SetCurrentStep](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwianimator_setcurrentstep.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `hwIAnimator_handle SetCurrentStep index` | This command sets the current animation step index. | **Inputs:** index The animation step index. |
| [hwIAnimator SetCurrentTime](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwianimator_setcurrenttime.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `hwIAnimator_handle SetCurrentTime sec` | This command sets the current time. | **Inputs:** sec The current time in seconds. |
| [hwIAnimator SetEndTime](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwianimator_setendtime.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `hwIAnimator_handle SetEndTime sec` | This command sets the end time. | **Inputs:** sec The end time in seconds. |
| [hwIAnimator SetNumberOfFrames](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwianimator_setnumberofframes.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `hwIAnimator_handle SetNumberOfFrames nframes` | This command sets the number of frames to be interpolated. | **Inputs:** nframes The number of frames to interpolate. |
| [hwIAnimator SetStartFrame](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwianimator_setstartframe.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `animator_handle SetStartFrame frame_idx` | This command sets the start frame for the animation. This is commonly used when the animation mode is modal. | **Inputs:** frame_idx Frame index is 0 based. |
| [hwIAnimator SetStartTime](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwianimator_setstarttime.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `hwIAnimator_handle SetStartTime sec` | This command sets the start time. | **Inputs:** sec The start time in seconds. |
| [hwIAnimator SetUseBitmap](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/hwianimator_setusebitmap.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `hwIAnimator_handle SetUseBitmap usebitmap` | This command sets the current animator's use bitmap flag. Note: This flag is used to speed up animation of large models by saving bitmap images for each frame, then flipping through them instead of recalculating each frame. | **Inputs:** usebitmap The use bitmap state: either true or false. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl Fit](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_fit.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle Fit` | This command fits the model(s) in the current window so that all of the model(s) is visible. | Not stated |
| [poI3DViewCtrl FitAllFrames](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_fitallframes.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle FitAllFrames` | This command fits the model(s) in the current window so that all of the model(s) is visible through all time steps. | Not stated |
| [poI3DViewCtrl RemoveView](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_remoteview.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle RemoveView view_name` | This command removes a memorized view from the memorized views list. | **Inputs:** view_name The name of the view. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl RenameView](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_renameview.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle RenameView old_view_name new_view_name` | This command renames an existing memorized view with a new name.Note: The new view name must be unique. | **Inputs:** old_view_name The name of the existing/old view. new_view_name The name of the new view. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl RestoreView](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_restoreview.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle RestoreView view_name` | This command updates the current view settings with the memorized settings saved with the given name. | **Inputs:** view_name The name of the view. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl RotateX](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_rotatex.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle RotateX angle` | This command rotates the contents of the current window about the X axis. | **Inputs:** angle Degrees of rotation. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl RotateY](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_rotatey.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle RotateY angle` | This command rotates the contents of the current window about the Y axis. | **Inputs:** angle Degrees of rotation. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl RotateZ](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_rotatez.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle RotateZ angle` | This command rotates the contents of the current window about the Z axis. | **Inputs:** angle Degrees of rotation. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SaveView](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_saveview.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle SaveView view_name` | This command memorizes the current view settings with a given name. If no name is specified, a default name will be given for the view. | **Inputs:** view_name The name of the view. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetActiveView](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setactiveview.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle SetActiveView active name` | In order to edit a stored view, it must be marked as active. Only one view, other than the current view, can be marked active. Whatever is currently displayed in the window will always be the same as the active view. | **Inputs:** active Boolean value that specifies whether to activate or deactivate a view. name Specifies the name of the view to be activated. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetAlignedImagePlane](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setalignedimageplane.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle SetAlignedImagePlane value name` | Set an ID of image planes to indicate that image plane is associated with this view. Information for the image plane with this ID is retrieved for recalculating view properties given the cropping situation. | **Inputs:** value The image plane ID. Any existing image plane IDs are valid inputs. If it is set to 0, it indicates that this view is not associated with any image plane. name String specifying the name of the view to be modified. The current view will be modified if a name is not specified. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetCropping](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setcropping.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle SetCropping values name` | Set the number of pixels pre-cropped from each corresponding side of the image plane. | **Inputs:** values A string consisting of top, bottom, left, and right values (in the format of “top bottom left right”). Captured videos/images are often processed before using them for video overlaying, one typical process is cropping the borders of a video/image. If such an operation has been taken, cropping information has to be provided before video overlaying. Otherwise, input sensor height and input principal point offset (also known as focal point offset) will not match the current status of the input video/image (that is, video overlaying will not provide correct results). name String specifying the name of the view to be modified. The current view will be modified if a name is not specified. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetFocalLength](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setfocallength.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle SetFocalLength value name` | The focal length value simulates the focal length of a camera lens. Adjusting this value is similar to zooming. | **Inputs:** value Float value specifying focal length. name String specifying the name of the view to be modified. If a name is not specified, the current view will be modified. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetInputPrincipalPointOffset](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setinputprincipalpointoffset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle SetInputPrincipalPointOffset values name` | Sets input values that represent a shift of the view horizontally and vertically in the window. | **Inputs:** values A string consisting of an x and y offset (in the format of “x y”). The values are typically the same as the manufacturer specifications of a camera. name A string specifying the name of the view to be modified. The current view will be modified if a name is not specified. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetInputSensorHeight](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setinputsensorheight.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle SetInputSensorHeight height name` | Sets the input value of the sensor height of a camera lens. | **Inputs:** height Value in mm of a simulated lens sensor value (must be greater than 0). This value is typically the same as the manufacturer specifications of a camera lens. name String specifying the name of the view to be modified. The current view will be modified if a name is not specified. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetLookAt](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setlookat.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle SetLookAt points name` | Uses three vertices to calculate and set the view matrix. | **Inputs:** points A string containing nine numbers. The first three numbers represent the position of the camera (eye). The next three numbers represent the position that the camera is looking at (center). The last three numbers represent the orientation of the camera (up). name String specifying the name of the view to be modified. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetOrientation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setorientation.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `viewControl_handle SetOrientation "Tx Ty Tz E1 E2 E3" Or viewControl_handle SetOrientation "<named_views>"` | Sets the orientation of the model within the view. Six floats are required: three for the translation of the model in X, Y, and Z, and three Euler angles for the rotation about the Z, X, Z axes. This command is valid for both the orthographic projection and perspective projection modes. | **Inputs:** Tx, Ty, Tz Translation of the model in X, Y, and Z. E1, E2, E3 Euler angles for the rotation of the model about Z, X, Z axes. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetOrtho](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setortho.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `viewControl_handle SetOrtho MinX, MaxX, MinY, MaxY, MinZ, MaxZ` | Redefines the view when in the orthogonal projection mode. | **Inputs:** MinX, MaxX Min and max clipping planes for X. MinY, MaxY Min and max clipping planes for Y. MinZ, MaxZ Min and max clipping planes for Z. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetPerspectiveAngle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setperspectiveangle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle SetPerspectiveAngle value name` | Sets the perspective angle of a stored view using a specified float number. | **Inputs:** value Float value specifying the perspective angle. name String specifying the name of the view to be modified. If a name is not specified, the current view will be modified. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetPerspectiveMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setperspectivemode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle SetPerspectiveMode` | This command turns the perspective mode on or off. | Not stated |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetPrincipalPointOffset](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setprincipalpointoffset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle SetPrincipalPointOffset values name` | Sets values that represent a shift of the view horizontally and vertically in the window. | **Inputs:** values A string consisting of an x and y offset (which should be in the "x y" format). name String specifying the name of the view to be modified. If a name is not specified, the current view will be modified. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetProjectionType](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setprojectiontype.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle SetProjectionType type name` | Orthographic and Perspective are the traditional HyperView modes. The Lens type simulates a camera lens. Different modes will apply the view volume/clipping region differently. | **Inputs:** type Set the projection type as Orthographic, Perspective, or Lens. name String specifying the name of the view to be modified. If a name is not specified, the current view will be modified. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetSensorHeight](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setsensorheight.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle SetSensorHeight height name` | This command sets the sensor height of a stored view. This value simulates the sensor height of a camera lens. | **Inputs:** height Value in mm of simulated lens sensor height. This value must be greater than 0. name String specifying the name of the view to be modified. If a name is not specified, the current view will be modified. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetUpOrientationMethod](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setuporientationmethod.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `viewControl_handle SetUpOrientationMethod type name` | Sets the orientation method of the camera top position. | **Inputs:** type Any of the following seven values can be used: x-up/x-down/y-up/y-down/z-up/z-down/vector. name String specifying the name of the view to be modified. The current view will be modified if a name is not specified. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetUpOrientationVector](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setuporientationvector.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `viewControl_handle SetUpOrientationVector vector name` | Sets the orientation vector of the camera top position. | **Inputs:** vector String containing three values defining the orientation vector. name String specifying the name of the view to be queried. The current view will be queried if a name is not specified. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetViewMatrix](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setviewmatrix.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle SetViewMatrix view_matrix` | This command updates the view orientation matrix of the current active window with the given sixteen float values. | **Inputs:** view_matrix The sixteen float values (a 4x4 matrix of space separated float values) which determine the view of the current active model. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetWithMasking](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setwithmasking.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle SetWithMasking with_masking name` | Sets the state of the "With Masking" flag for the view with the given name. If True, the masking state will be saved within the view. If False, the masking state will not be saved within the view. | **Inputs:** with_masking True to enable saving the masking state as part of the view. False to disable saving the masking state as part of the view. name The name of the saved view. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl SetZOffset](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_setzoffset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle SetZOffset offset name` | This command sets the z offset of a stored view. | **Inputs:** offset This value represents the distance from the near to far clipping planes which affect the perspective. It will have no effect in the orthographic projection. name String specifying the name of the view to be modified. If a name is not specified, the current view will be modified. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl Translate](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_translate.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle Translate x y z` | This command translates the contents of the current window to the given position. | **Inputs:** x The X position. y The Y position. z The Z position. |
| [poI3DViewCtrl Zoom](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poi3dviewctrl_zoom.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poI3DViewCtrl_handle Zoom factor` | This command zooms by the given factor. | **Inputs:** factor Positive value zooms in; < 1.0 value zooms out. |
| [poIAdvancedQuery AddSimulations](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiadvancedquery_addsimulations.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `advancedQuery_handle AddSimulations "subcases" "simulations"` | This command is used to add/load simulations for the given subcases in advanced query. | **Inputs:** subcases Holds the subcase information which accepts “current”, “first”, “last”, “all” or the space separated string with hwIDs for a subcase ID (for example: "1 2 3 4"). simulations Holds the simulations information which accepts “current”, “first”, “last”, “all” or the space separated string with hwIDs for a simulation ID (for example: "1 2 3 4"). |
| [poIAdvancedQuery CreateBestViews](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiadvancedquery_createbestviews.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `advancedQuery_handle CreateBestViews` | This command creates BestView entries for every hotspot found by the advanced query. The numbering of BestViews follows a global list of Best Views under the post handle. | Not stated |
| [poIAdvancedQuery RemoveSimulations](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiadvancedquery_removesimulations.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `advancedQuery_handle RemoveSimulations "subcases" "simulations"` | This command is used to remove the list of simulations for a given subcase from the advanced query. | **Inputs:** subcases Holds the subcase information which accepts “current”, “first”, “last”, “all” or the space separated string with hwIDs for a subcase ID (for example: "1 2 3 4"). simulations Holds the simulation information which accepts “current”, “first”, “last”, “all” or the space separated string with hwIDs for a simulation ID (for example: "1 2 3 4"). |
| [poIAdvancedQuery SetLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiadvancedquery_setlabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `advancedQuery_handle SetLabel label` | This command sets the label to the advanced query. | **Inputs:** label The label of the advanced query. |
| [poIAdvancedQuery SetNumericFormat](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiadvancedquery_setnumericformat.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `advancedQuery_handle SetNumericFormat format` | This command is used to set the numeric format to the advanced query to get the formatted output values. | **Inputs:** format Accepted formats include: fixed, scientific, and engineering. |
| [poIAdvancedQuery SetNumericPrecision](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiadvancedquery_setnumericprecision.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `advancedQuery_handle SetNumericPrecision precision` | This command is used to set the numeric precision to the advanced query. | **Inputs:** precision Accepts the precision number which is ideally an integer value from 0 to 12. |
| [poIAdvancedQuery SetQuery](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiadvancedquery_setquery.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `advancedQuery_handle SetQuery query` | This command is used to set the query string in the advanced query. | **Inputs:** query Accepts only the following query strings: “entity.id”, “entity.pool”, “entity.value”, “entity.layer”, “component.id”, “component.name”, “subcase.id”, “subcase.label”, “simulation.id”, “simulation.label”, “elem.config” and “corner.data”. |
| [poIAdvancedQuery SetQueryLogic](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiadvancedquery_setquerylogic.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `advancedQuery_handle SetQueryLogic rule` | This command sets the rule to the advanced query. | **Inputs:** rule The rule ID in the string format. |
| [poIAdvancedQuery SetScalarDefine](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiadvancedquery_setscalardefine.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `advancedQuery_handle SetScalarDefine id` | This command sets the ID of the scalar define. | **Inputs:** id The scalar define ID. |
| [poIAdvancedQuery SetSearchOption](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiadvancedquery_setsearchoption.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `advancedQuery_handle SetSearchOption name state` | This command is used to set the status of the search options for the advanced query. The output will be calculated based on the search options, for example: [SetSearchOption "simulation" true] will result in the output being displayed per simulation basis. [SetSearchOption "component" true] will result in the output being displayed per component. Tip: A result can also be combined with both options. | **Inputs:** name Valid name options: "simulation" or "component". state Valid options: true or false. |
| [poIAssembly SetIDVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiassembly_setidvisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `assem_handle SetIDVisibility state` | This command sets the display state of the assembly ID (on or off). | **Inputs:** state True or False. |
| [poIAssembly SetLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiassembly_setlabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIAssembly_handle SetLabel label` | This command sets the assembly label. | **Inputs:** label The ASCII label. |
| [poIAssembly SetParentID](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiassembly_setparentid.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `assem_handle SetParentID id` | This command sets/changes the parent of an assembly to a different parent ID. | **Inputs:** id The new parent assembly ID. |
| [poIAssembly SetVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiassembly_setvisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `assem_handle SetVisibility state` | This command sets the visibility state of an assembly. | **Inputs:** state True if visible, or false if not. |
| [poICFD AddRake](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_addrake.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_handle AddRake rake_type` | This command adds a new rake to be used in the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel (streamline creation), and returns the ID of the newly added rake (if successful). The rake type must be one of the supported rake types. You can get the supported rake types from the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel by using the GetRakeTypeList command. | **Inputs:** rake_type The rake type to be set. |
| [poICFD CreateStreamLines](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_createstreamlines.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_handle CreateStreamLines rake_ids` | This command creates streamlines for all rakes specified by the rake_ids argument, based on the current settings in the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel. If no argument is passed, this command will create streamlines for all of the existing rakes. The rake_ids argument should contain the rake IDs, separated by a space or comma. Any invalid rake IDs specified in the list will be ignored. | **Inputs:** rake_ids The ID list of the rakes for which the streamlines are to be created. |
| [poICFD DeleteRake](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_deleterake.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_handle DeleteRake rake_id` | This command deletes the rake specified by the rake_id from the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel (streamline creation). The rake specified by the rake_id must exist. You can get the IDs of the existing rakes from the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel by using the GetRakeList command. | **Inputs:** rake_id The ID of the rake to be deleted. |
| [poICFD DeleteStreamLines](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_deletestreamlines.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_handle DeleteStreamLines rake_ids` | This command deletes the streamlines belonging to all of the rakes specified by the rake_ids in the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel. If no argument is passed, this command will delete the streamlines for all of the existing rakes. The rake_ids argument should contain the rake IDs, separated by a space or comma. Any invalid rake IDs specified in the list will be ignored. | **Inputs:** rake_ids The ID list of the rakes for which the streamlines are to be deleted. |
| [poICFD SetDataType](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_setdatatype.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_handle SetDataType data_type_name` | This command sets the data type to be used for the streamline creation in the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel. Although in most cases nodal velocity vector data is the only sensible data type which can be used to create streamlines properly, HyperView does allow you to select all the nodal vector data types available in the result file for the purpose of streamline creation. If the data type name has spaces in it, then the name has to be specified inside double quotes (for example, “Velocity Field”). You can get this list from the result control by using the GetDataTypeList command with the CFD argument. | **Inputs:** data_type_name The name of the data type to be set. |
| [poICFD SetIntegrationMethod](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_setintegrationmethod.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_handle SetIntegrationMethod integration_method` | This command specifies the integration algorithm used for the streamline creation in the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel. The specified Integration_method must be one of the supported modes. You can get a list of supported integration methods from the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel by using the GetIntegrationMethodList command. | **Inputs:** integration_method The integration method for the streamline creation to be set. |
| [poICFD SetMaxBreakAngle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_setmaxbreakangle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_handle SetMaxBreakAngle break_angle` | This command specifies the threshold value for the break angle between two neighboring streamline segments, which is used to decide if the two line segments should be merged into one, or kept untouched. This command will only take effect when streamline decimation is turned on by calling the SetStreamLineDecimation command. The valid value range for the break_angle is from 0 to 90 degrees. | **Inputs:** break_angle The break angle threshold for decimation to be set. |
| [poICFD SetMaxStepLength](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_setmaxsteplength.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_handle SetMaxStepLength step_mode step_length` | This command specifies the maximum integration step length used in the process of the streamline creation in the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel, and returns success (if successful). Note that the step length actually used in the integration could be smaller than the value specified by this command, and that the step_mode must be on of the supported modes. You can get a list of supported modes from the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel by calling the GetIntegrationStepLengthModeList command. | **Inputs:** step_mode The mode in which the maximum step length is defined. The only current valid mode is Element-fraction, which specifies that the maximum integration step length is defined based on a fraction of the current element size (the diagonal of the bounding box of the element). step_length The value used to define the maximum integration step length. The step mode of Element-fraction specifies the maximum step length as a fraction of the current element size. The minimum valid value is 0.002. |
| [poICFD SetNumOfStreamLineColors](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_setnumofstreamlinecolors.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_handle SetNumOfStreamLineColors num_streamline_colors` | This command specifies the number of colors in the color map used to color the individual streamlines in the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel. This command will only take effect when the 'colored-by mode' is set to “seed”. The valid range of num_streamline_colors is from 1 to 14. Note that if the number of streamlines belonging to the same rake exceeds the number of the colors set by this command, the colors will be recycled. This command will be ignored if a contour plot is enabled. | **Inputs:** Num_streamline_colors The number of colors in the color map used for streamline coloring to be set. |
| [poICFD SetStreamLineColoredByMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_setstreamlinecoloredbymode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_handle SetStreamLineColoredByMode streamline_colored_by_mode` | This command specifies how to color the streamlines in the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel, and returns success (if successful). The valid streamline 'colored-by modes' are “seed” and “rake”. If per “seed” is specified, the individual streamlines will be colored in different colors. If per “rake” is specified, all of the streamlines belonging to the same rake will have the same color. Note: This command will be ignored if a contour plot is enabled. | **Inputs:** streamline_colored_by_mode The streamline 'colored-by mode' to be set. |
| [poICFD SetStreamLineDecimation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_setstreamlinedecimation.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_handle SetStreamLineDecimation decimation_on` | This command specifies whether or not to apply decimation to the streamlines obtained by the integration process. Decimation will effectively reduce the memory footprint and improve graphic performance afterwards, however it is important to note that the streamlines could become less smooth and decimation process itself takes some time. After decimation is turned on, you can further specify decimation criterion based on max break angle, max speed variation, and max line segment length by calling the following commands: SetMaxBreakAngle, SetMaxSpeedVariation, and SetMaxLineSegmentLength. | **Inputs:** decimation_on The boolean value turning decimation on or off. |
| [poICFD SetStreamLineSize](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_setstreamlinesize.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_handle SetStreamLineSize streamline_size` | This command specifies the size of the streamlines in the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel, and returns success (if successful). The streamline size must be a positive float. | **Inputs:** streamline_size The streamline size to be set. |
| [poICFD SetStreamLineStyle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_setstreamlinestyle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_handle SetStreamLineStyle streamline_style` | This command specifies the style of the streamlines in the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel, and returns success (if successful). The streamline type must be one of the supported streamline styles. You can get the supported streamline styles from the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel by using the GetStreamLineStyleList command. | **Inputs:** streamline_style The streamline style to be set. |
| [poICFD SetTerminationCriterion](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_setterminationcriterion.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_handle SetTerminationCriterion termination_mode termination_value` | This command specifies when the integration of the streamline creation should be forced to stop. There are different modes to define such termination criterion, however the termination_mode argument should be one of the supported modes. You can get a list of supported integration termination criterion modes from the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel by using the GetTerminationCriteriaList command. Note: You can specify multiple criteria at the same time. The integration will stop whenever any one of the criteria is satisfied. | **Inputs:** termination_mode The mode used to define the integration termination criterion. The valid modes are: max_length Used to limit the maximum length of an individual streamline. Valid values are any positive floats. max_num_steps Used to limit the total number of integration steps. Valid values are any non-zero nature numbers. terminate_speed Used to set the terminate speed. Allows you to inform HyperView to terminate the integration for the current streamline when the local speed (magnitude of velocity) is under a certain value, since such a speed is deemed as too slow to be meaningful for streamline creation. Note that terminate_speed specifies the speed as a percentage of the medium speed of the whole fluid field. You can get the medium speed from the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel by calling the GetMediumSpeed command The valid value range is from 0 - 100. termination_value The value used to define the integration termination criterion. |
| [poICFD_rake SetIntegrationMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_rake_setintegrationmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_rake_handle SetIntegrationMode integration_mode` | This command specifies the integration mode for the rake specified by the rake handle in the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel. The specified Integration_mode must be one of the supported modes. You can get a list of supported integration methods from the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel by using the GetIntegrationModeList command with a CFD Plot/Streamlines panel handle. | **Inputs:** integration_mode The integration mode for the streamline creation to be set. |
| [poICFD_rake SetLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_rake_setlabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_rake_handle SetLabel rake_label` | This command sets the label of the rake in the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel. | **Inputs:** rake_label The label to be set for the rake. |
| [poICFD_rake SetNumberOfSeeds](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_rake_setnumberofseeds.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_rake_handle SetNumberOfSeeds num_seeds` | This command specifies the number of the seeds in the rake used for the streamline creation in the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel and returns success (if successful). Note: In the case of an "Area" rake, the number of seeds actually created could be smaller than the value specified by this command, since the real number of seeds cannot exceed the number of elements in the 2D part, which is specified when the rake is defined. The Num_seeds argument must be a positive integer. | **Inputs:** num_seeds The number of seeds in the rake. |
| [poICFD_rake SetRakeGoemetry](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicfd_rake_setrakegeometry.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICFD_rake_handle SetRakeGeometry "rake_def_parameters" "rake_type"` | This command sets the definition of the rake in the CFD Plot/Streamlines panel. If the rake_type argument is specified, the rake will change to the new type and the rake_def_parameters argument will be parsed and interpreted based on the new rake type. | **Inputs:** rake_def_parameters This argument is used to: Specify the 2D part used as the seeds ‘carrier’ - in the case of an "Area" rake type which is in the format of a part locator string or define the positions of the rake start/end points - in the case of a "Line" rake type which in the following format: X1, Y1, Z1,X2, Y2, Z2.Note: Spaces may be used as separators also. |
| [poICollisionCtrl AddCollisionSet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicollisionctrl_addcollisionset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICollisionCtrl_handle AddCollisionSet` | This command adds a collision set to be used by the collision control. | Not stated |
| [poICollisionCtrl RemoveCollisionSet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicollisionctrl_removecollisionset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICollisionCtrl_handle RemoveCollisionSet` | This command removes the collision set. | Not stated |
| [poICollisionCtrl Reset](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicollisionctrl_reset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICollisionCtrl_handle Reset` | This command clears all collision sets. | Not stated |
| [poICollisionCtrl SetActiveCollisionSet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicollisionctrl_setactivecollisionset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICollisionCtrl_handle SetActiveCollisionSet` | This command sets the active collision set value. | Not stated |
| [poICollisionCtrl SetEnableState](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicollisionctrl_setenablestate.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICollisionCtrl_handle SetEnableState state` | This command sets the auto load/unload collision data state. Setting enable state to false causes currently loaded data to be unloaded. | **Inputs:** state True If collision data can be loaded. False If collision data cannot be loaded. |
| [poICollisionCtrl WriteSummary](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicollisionctrl_writesummary.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICollisionCtrl_handle WriteSummary file` | This command writes out the summary for the collision detection. | **Inputs:** file The file where the summary should be written to. |
| [poICollisionSet AddSelectionSet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicollisionset_addselectionset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICollisionSet_handle AddSelectionSet` | This command adds selected entities to be used by the collision control. | Not stated |
| [poICollisionSet RemoveSelectionSet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicollisionset_removeselectionset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICollisionSet_handle RemoveSelectionSet` | This command removes the selected entities. | Not stated |
| [poICollisionSet Reset](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicollisionset_reset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICollisionSet_handle Reset` | This command clears all selection sets. | Not stated |
| [poICollisionSet SetAnimationEvent](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicollisionset_setanimationevent.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICollisionSet_handle SetAnimationEvent` | This command sets the event which triggers animation stop. | Not stated |
| [poICollisionSet SetBinding](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicollisionset_setbinding.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICollisionSet_handle SetBinding` | This command sets the binding: part, element, or node. | Not stated |
| [poICollisionSet SetEnableProximity](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicollisionset_setenableproximity.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICollisionSet_handle SetEnableProximity` | This command sets the proximity state. | Not stated |
| [poICollisionSet SetLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicollisionset_setlabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICollisionSet_handle SetLabel label` | This command sets the collision set label. | **Inputs:** label The collision set label. |
| [poICollisionSet SetProximity](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicollisionset_setproximity.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICollisionSet_handle SetProximity` | This command sets the proximity value. | Not stated |
| [poIComplexCtrl SetAnimationFilter](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicomplexctrl_setanimationfilter.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIComplexCtrl_handle SetAnimationFilter` | This command sets the current complex filter for the current animation mode. | Not stated |
| [poIComplexCtrl SetDefaultFilter](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicomplexctrl_setdefaultfilter.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIComplexCtrl_handle SetDefaultFilter` | This command sets the current result filter to the default state. | Not stated |
| [poIComponent GetTranslation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicomponent_gettranslation.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIComponent_handle GetTranslation` | This command returns the translation vector in the global coordinate system. | Not stated |
| [poIComponent GetTranslation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicomponent_gettranslation_2.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIComponent_handle GetTranslation` | This command returns the translation vector in the global coordinate system. | Not stated |
| [poIComponent SetAssemblyID](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicomponent_setassemblyid.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIComponent_handle SetAssemblyID value` | This command sets the assembly ID of a component. | **Inputs:** value The assembly ID to be set. |
| [poIComponent SetAssemblyID](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicomponent_setassemblyid_2.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIComponent_handle SetAssemblyID value` | This command sets the assembly ID of a component. | **Inputs:** value The assembly ID to be set. |
| [poIComponent SetAutoColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicomponent_setautocolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIComponent_handle SetAutoColor index` | This command sets the component’s auto color index. | **Inputs:** index The color index. |
| [poIComponent SetAutoColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicomponent_setautocolor_2.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIComponent_handle SetAutoColor index` | This command sets the component’s auto color index. | **Inputs:** index The color index. |
| [poIComponent SetColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicomponent_setcolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIComponent_handle SetColor str` | This command sets the component’s color index. | **Inputs:** str The color identifier string. |
| [poIComponent SetColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicomponent_setcolor_2.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIComponent_handle SetColor str` | This command sets the component’s color index. | **Inputs:** str The color identifier string. |

| [poIContourCtrl RenamePlotStyle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_renameplotstyle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle RenamePlotStyle old_style_name new_style_name` | This command renames an existing/old plot style with a new name.Note: The new plot style name must be unique. | **Inputs:** old_style_name The name of the old/existing plot style. new_style_name The new name to be given to the old/existing plot style. |
| [poIContourCtrl RestorePlotStyle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_restoreplotstyle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle RestorePlotStyle style_name` | This command updates the contour controller settings with the new settings specified by the plot style name. | **Inputs:** style_name The name of the plot style. |
| [poIContourCtrl SavePlotStyle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_saveplotstyle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SavePlotStyle style_name` | This command saves the current contour controller settings with the name specified. | [poIContourCtrl SetAverageColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setaveragecolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetAverageColor enable` | This command sets the averaging state for element results. When enabled, elemental colors are averaged to nodes (color per vertex).Note: Color averaging applies only to elemental values. Enabling averaging has no affect on values reported. | **Inputs:** enable Set to true to enable color per vertex, or false to disable color averaging. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetAverageFactor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setaveragefactor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetAverageFactor` | This command sets the valid range for the nodal variation factor. Note: Specify the threshold variation level above which the nodal averaging should not be carried out. | Not stated |
| [poIContourCtrl SetAverageMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setaveragemode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetAverageMode mode` | This command specifies the method by which elemental results are averaged to nodes. | **Inputs:** mode Values can be: none, simple, maximum, minimum, advanced, difference, Max of corner, Min of corner, or Extreme of corner. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetAvgAcrossPartsEnable](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setavgacrosspartsenable.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetAvgAcrossPartsEnable state` | This command sets the state of the average across parts. | **Inputs:** state Boolean value to set the average across parts state to True or False. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetColoredByLayer](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setcoloredbylayer.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetColoredByLayer (bool color_mode)` | This command sets the current color mode. If the argument is True, the color mode is set to colored-by-layer mode (which means each layer is rendered in a different color). Otherwise, it sets the color mode to regular rendering. | Not stated |
| [poIContourCtrl SetCornerDataEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setcornerdataenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetCornerDataEnabled enable` | This command enables contouring node on element data. Note: This state is valid only if the data binding is node-on-element. | **Inputs:** enable Set to true to enable corner data processing, or false to average corner data to the element centroid. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetCurrentPlotStyle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setcurrentplotstyle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetCurrentPlotStyle "style_name"` | This command updates the current contour plot style with the given value. | **Inputs:** style_name The name of the plot style. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetDataComponent](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setdatacomponent.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetDataComponent dim comp_label` | This command sets the data component for a given component.Note: The component dimension must be enabled before setting. | **Inputs:** dim The dimension. Options include "node", "line", "shell", or "solid". comp_label The component label. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetDataType](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setdatatype.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `contour_handle SetDataType data_type_name` | This command sets the data type to be applied for a contour plot (for example, Stress, Velocity, etc.). If the data type name contains spaces, then the name must be specified inside double quotes (for example, "Effective plastic strain"). The data type name must exist in the list of available data types in the results file in order to display a proper contour plot. You can obtain a list of valid data types from the result control by using the GetDataTypeList command. | **Inputs:** data_type_name The name of the data type to be set. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetDimensionEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setdimensionenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetDimensionEnabled dim_label state` | This command sets the dimension state; enabled or disabled. | **Inputs:** dim_label The dimension state. Options include "line", "shell", or "solid". state True if enabled, false if not. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetDiscreteColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setdiscretecolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetDiscreteColor enable` | This command sets the discrete color mode for node-based coloring.Note: This option is valid only for nodal, node-on-element, or averaged elemental data. | **Inputs:** enable Set to true for discrete contour coloring, or false for blended RGB contour coloring. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetEnabled pool state` | This command sets the dimension state, either enabled or disabled. | **Inputs:** pool The pool from which to set the enable state. state True if enabled; false if disabled. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetEnableState](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setenablestate.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetEnableState state` | This command sets the auto load/unload contour data state. Note: Setting the enable state to false causes currently loaded data to be unloaded. | **Inputs:** state True if contour data can be loaded; otherwise false. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetFeatureAngleAverage](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setfeatureangleaverage.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `contour_handle SetFeatureAngleAverage angle` | This command sets the angle for the feature angle average. The specified angle should be between 0 and 180 degrees. | **Inputs:** angle The angle for the feature angle average. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetFeatureAngleAverageEnableState](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setfeatureangleaverageenablestate.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `contour_handle SetFeatureAngleAverageEnableState state` | This command sets the state of the feature angle average. | **Inputs:** state Boolean value to set the feature angle average state (True or False). |
| [poIContourCtrl SetIfMultiLayer](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setifmultilayer.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetIfMultiLayer bool` | This command sets the Top/Bottom layer mode in the contour controller. True enables the mode and allows you to select multiple layers. | **Inputs:** bool Boolean value. Set to True to enable the Top/Bottom mode, or False to disable it. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetIgnoredFlippedNormal](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setignoredflippednormal.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `contour_handle SetIgnoreFlippedNormal state` | This command sets the state of the "Ignore Flipped Normal" option for the feature angle average. | **Inputs:** state A Boolean value to set the "Ignore Flipped Normal" state (True or False). |
| [poIContourCtrl SetInterpolateColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setinterpolatecolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetInterpolateColor enable` | This command sets the interpolate color mode for Interpolation/Modal animation mode. Note: This option is valid only for Interpolation/Modal mode. | **Inputs:** enable Set to true to vary color with interpolation frame, or false to show result value for all interpolation frames. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetLayer](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setlayer.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetLayer layer` | This command sets the current layer label. For this multiple layer support, the string name passed should be “Top/Bottom”. If not, multiple layer will be disabled and ends up setting the layer name which is being passed. | **Inputs:** layer The shell layer label or one of the following derived types: max Stores the maximum value of all layers. min Stores the minimum value of all layers. extreme Stores the maximum absolute value or all layers. sum Stores the (scalar) sum of all values among the layers for each entity. average Stores the (scalar) average value among the layers which contain data for each entity. range Stores the difference between the maximum and minimum values among the layers for each entity. maxlayer Stores the layer number (instead of the actual value) which contributed to the maximum value for each entity. minlayer Stores the layer number (instead of the actual value) which contributed to the minimum value for each entity. extremelayer Stores the layer number (instead of the actual value) which contributed to the maximum values for each entity. Note: Only in the case of results based at the elemental centroid, would one layer per element be displayed. For corner bound or nodal bound results (in the case of averaging), different layers may contribute to the max/min/extreme value at each corner/node of the same element. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetMultipleLayerList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setmultiplelayerlist.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetMultipleLayeList std::string` | This command sets the Top and Bottom layer. The Top and Bottom layer are taken in a form of a single string with delimiters. The Delimiters can be “,;\\|”. | **Inputs:** std::string The Top/Bottom layer information is set in the form of a string with delimiters. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetMultiplier](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setmultiplier.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `contour_handle SetMultiplier mult_factor` | This command sets a multiplier for the applied contour plot (for example: Stress, Velocity, etc.). The multiplication factor should be a positive value. The original contour value will be multiplied with the specified multiplication factor. The legend will reflect the new contour values.Note: Setting a multiplication factor of 2 for 3 times will not scale the original value by 6. The multiplication factor always uses the original value for scaling, rather than the previously multiplied value. | **Inputs:** mult_factor Multiplication factor to be used. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetOffset](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setoffset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetOffset value` | This command sets the offset value of the contour. | **Inputs:** value Offset value to be set. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetResultSystem](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setresultsystem.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetResultSystem system_id` | This command sets the result system ID. Note: Setting the result system is valid only for transformable data types, such as vector tensor. | **Inputs:** system_id The result system ID or one of the following special identifiers: 0 global -1 analysis -2 elemental -3 tracking -4 material -5 ply |
| [poIContourCtrl SetSelectionSet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setselectionset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetSelectionSet set_id` | This command specifies the selected entities to be used by the contour control. | **Inputs:** set_id The set ID. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetShellLayer](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setshelllayer.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetShellLayer layer` | This command sets the current shell layer. The scalar storage within HyperView can accept a shell layer or a derived quantity of all shell layers (max, min, or extreme). | **Inputs:** layer The shell layer label or one of the following derived types: max Stores the maximum value of all layers. min Stores the minimum value of all layers. extreme Stores the maximum absolute value of all layers. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetShowMidsideNodeResults](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setshowmidsidenoderesults.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `contour_handle SetShowMidsideNodeResults state` | This command sets the state of the Show Midside Node Results option for the contour control. | **Inputs:** state Boolean value to set the "Show Midside Node Results" state to true or false. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetThresholdMax](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setthresholdmax.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetThresholdMax value` | This command sets the maximum threshold value of a contour.Note: If an invalid real value is passed, then the value is set to 0.0. | **Inputs:** value The maximum threshold value to be set. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetThresholdMaxEnableState](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setthresholdmaxenablestate.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetThresholdMaxEnableState value` | This command sets the threshold maximum enable state value of a contour (true/false). | **Inputs:** value True/false. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetThresholdMin](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setthresholdmin.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetThresholdMin value` | This command sets the minimum threshold value of a contour.Note: If an invalid real value is passed, then the value is set to 0.0. | **Inputs:** value Min threshold value to be set. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetThresholdMinEnableState](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setthresholdminenablestate.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIContourCtrl_handle SetThresholdMinEnableState value` | This command sets the threshold minimum enable state value of a contour (true/false). | **Inputs:** value True/false. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetValueFilter](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setvaluefilter.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `contour_handle SetValueFilter filter` | This command sets the value filter for the contour. | **Inputs:** filter The filter string to be applied. Examples of the valid filter strings are: “>= 10.123”, “<= 10.123”, and “Range 10.123 100.456”. |
| [poIContourCtrl SetValueFilterEnableState](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicontourctrl_setvaluefilterenablestate.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `contour_handle SetValueFilterEnableState state` | This command sets the value filter state for the contour. | **Inputs:** state The boolean value to set the value filter state (True or False). |
| [poICrossSectionPlanar MakeReadOnly](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicrosssectionplanar_makereadonly.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICrossSectionPlanar_handle MakeReadOnly value` | This command makes a cross section read only. | **Inputs:** value (bool) True to make read only, otherwise false. |

| [poICrossSectionPlanar SetBase](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicrosssectionplanar_setbase.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICrossSectionPlanar_handle SetBase base_coord` | This command sets the cross section base coordinate. | **Inputs:** base_coord The base coordinate as a string. |
| [poICrossSectionPlanar SetClipAbove](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicrosssectionplanar_setclipabove.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICrossSectionPlanar_handle SetClipAbove state` | This command toggles the current direction of the clipping surface for the cross section. | [poICrossSectionPlanar SetColorOverride](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poicrosssectionplanar_setcoloroverride.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poICrossSectionPlanar_handle SetColorOverride override` | This command sets the sate of the color override option for a cross section. | **Inputs:** override Set to 'true' to change (override) the section color, otherwise 'false'. |

| [poIGraphicMaterial SetShininess](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poigraphicmaterial_setshininess.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIGraphicMaterial_handle SetShininess shininess` | This command sets the material shininess value. Note: Values approaching 1.0 simulate a smooth, shiny surface; while values approaching 0.0 simulate a rough surface. | **Inputs:** shininess The shininess value for the material (0.0 to 1.0). |
| [poIGraphicMaterial SetSpecular](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poigraphicmaterial_setspecular.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIGraphicMaterial_handle SetSpecular specular` | This command sets the specular highlight color of the graphic material. Note: Values approaching 1.0 simulate a smooth, shiny surface; while values approaching 0.0 simulate a rough surface. | **Inputs:** shininess The specular color in a space-separated RGB string, ranging in value from 0.0 to 1.0. |
| [poIImagePlane AddAlignmentPoint](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_addalignmentpoint.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle AddAlignmentPoint` | This command adds an alignment point to an image plane. The alignment point at this stage is not fully defined (it does not contain model node or the image point definition associated with it). To fully define the alignment point, the “SetAlignmentPoint” command must be used. This command returns the index of the alignment point. Index starts with 0. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane CalcVidTracking](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_calcvidtracking.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle CalcVidTracking x y` | This command calculates the data needed to track an image plane video. The calculation is based on the x and y pixel coordinates. | **Inputs:** x The number of pixels from the left edge of the image to the tracking point. y The number of pixels from the top edge of the image to the tracking point. |
| [poIImagePlane ClearAlignmentPoints](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_clearalignmentpoints.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle ClearAlignmentPoints` | This command clears all the alignment points associated with an image plane. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane LocateBy2Points](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_locateby2points.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle LocateBy2Points pointList` | An image plane in 3D mode can be aligned with a model to check correlation between a video and a simulation. This command aligns the image plane by defining a plane in model space, followed by two points in model space, and two corresponding points on the image or video. The image plane is then scaled and positioned appropriately to match the points specified. | **Inputs:** pointList The alignment point list. The list consists of the X, Y, Z values of a vector defining a normal to the 3D plane on which the image plane will reside. This is followed by two X, Y, Z points in model space, and two corresponding X, Y points on the image. |
| [poIImagePlane LocateBy3Points](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_locateby3points.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle LocateBy3Points pointList` | An image plane in 3D mode can be aligned with a model to check correlation between a video and a simulation. This command aligns the image plane by defining three points on the model and three corresponding points on the video or image. The image plane is then scaled and positioned appropriately to match the points specified. | **Inputs:** pointList The alignment point list. The list consists of the X, Y, Z values for three points on the model, followed by three corresponding X, Y points on the image. |
| [poIImagePlane LocateByMultPoints](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_locatebymultpoints.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle LocateByMultPoints pointList execute` | This command aligns the model with an image plane. The model is transformed to match the perspective of an image. | **Inputs:** pointList String containing at least seven corresponding model and image points that would be used for an alignment The string is in the following format: # of points, model node locator strings, x y image points. This is the same format that is returned from GetAlignmentData when the alignment mode is set to IP_MULT_POINT. execute Boolean value set to true will calculate alignment and update the view. A false value will calculate alignment but not store or update the view, it is used to update alignment error in order to give users a sense of how good the current alignment points are. |
| [poIImagePlane ManipIdentity](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_manipidentity.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle ManipIdentity` | The image plane maintains a separate user transform to track the position, rotation, and scaling applied to the image plane. This command resets the transform, however it does not alter any data regarding alignment of a 3D image plane to a model. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane RemoveAlignmentPoint](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_removealignmentpoint.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle RemoveAlignmentPoint idx` | This command removes an alignment point from an image plane. | **Inputs:** idx The index of the alignment point to be deleted. Index starts at 0. |
| [poIImagePlane SetAlignmentPoint](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setalignmentpoint.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetAlignmentPoint idx mloc imgx imgy` | This command edits the alignment point of an image plane. | **Inputs:** idx The index of the alignment point to be deleted. Index starts at 0. mloc The node locator string for the model node. imgx The x pixel location of the image. imgy The y pixel location of the image. |
| [poIImagePlane SetAlpha](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setalpha.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetAlpha alpha` | This command sets the alpha or transparency setting for the image plane. Allowed values are from 0 – 100, where 100 is completely opaque, and 0 is transparent. The transparency of an image plane in 3D mode is also affected by the cull angle settings. | **Inputs:** Alpha The new alpha setting. |
| [poIImagePlane SetAssociatedModelID](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setassociatedmodelid.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetAssociatedModelID id` | When a tracking system is created and made active, it can be desirable for a 3D image plane to follow the tracking system.
