This command identifies which model, if any, the image plane should follow. This setting is only relevant if the image plane is in 3D mode, and if there is an active tracking system on that model. A value of -1 indicates no model is associated with the image plane. | **Inputs:** id A valid model ID. |
| [poIImagePlane SetBackFaceVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setbackfacevisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetBackFaceVisibility val` | When an image plane is in 3D mode, the model and image plane may get rotated so that the camera is looking at the back of the plane. The back face visibility setting determines whether the image plane remains visible when the back face is exposed. This command sets/modifies the back face visibility setting. | **Inputs:** val True or False to indicate whether back faces should be drawn. |
| [poIImagePlane SetColorAlphaTarget](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setcoloralphatarget.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetColorAlphaTarget mode colotStr` | The image plane offers several image post-processing filters that can be run on the image or video. The ColorAlpha filter makes the target color in the image or video transparent. This command specifies the target color. | **Inputs:** mode The filter mode name. Valid values are returned by GetFilterModeList. They are different for each filter type. colorStr The target color, specified as three values between 0 and 255, representing RGB components. |
| [poIImagePlane SetColorAlphaTolerance](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setcoloralphatolerance.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetColorAlphaTolderance tolerance` | The image plane offers several image post-processing filters that can be run on the image or video. The ColorAlpha filter makes the target color in the image or video transparent. This command specifies the search tolerance for the target color. This only applies when the filter mode is set to “equal”. | **Inputs:** tolerance The search tolerance, specified as an integer that represents a tolerance percentage applied to the color value when searching. |
| [poIImagePlane SetCrop](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setcrop.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetCrop minx miny maxx maxy` | The image or video can be cropped before being drawn in the HyperView window. This command specifies the crop settings for the image plane. The required values are the left, top, right, and bottom, and range in value between 0 and 1, expressed as a percentage of the image width or height that will be cropped from that edge.. No crop is expressed as “0.0 0.0 1.0 1.0”. | **Inputs:** minx The amount to crop from the left edge of the image. 0 means no crop. miny The amount to crop from the top edge of the image. 0 means no crop. maxx The amount to crop from the right edge of the image. 1.0 means no crop. maxy The amount to crop from the bottom edge of the image. 1.0 means no crop. |
| [poIImagePlane SetCullAngle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setcullangle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetCullAngle deg` | When the image plane mode is 3D, HyperView compares the camera view angle to the image plane’s surface normal. When the difference is greater than the cull angle, HyperView applies transparency to the image plane. The greater the angle difference, the more transparent it becomes. The transparency effect is also multiplied by the Alpha property of the image plane. This command sets/changes the current cull angle value. | **Inputs:** deg The new culling angle in degrees. |
| [poIImagePlane SetEdgeColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setedgecolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetEdgeColor colorStr` | The image plane offers several image post-processing filters that can be applied on the image or video. One of them is an edge detection filter, where detected edges in the image are rendered with a specified color. This command sets the RGB values for the color of the rendered edges. | **Inputs:** colorStr A set of RGB color values that define the color. A string containing three RGB values between 0 and 255 must be passed. |
| [poIImagePlane SetFileName](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setfilename.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetFileName filename` | Each image plane is associated with a image or video file. This command sets/specifies a new image or video that HyperView will open, process, and associate with the image plane. | **Inputs:** filename Specifies the complete path to the new media file to open. |
| [poIImagePlane SetFilterMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setfiltermode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetFilterMode mode` | The image plane offers several image post-processing filters that can be applied on the image or video. This command sets/changes the filter mode being used for the image plane. | **Inputs:** mode The filter mode name. Valid values are returned by GetFilterModeList. They are different for each filter type. For the Edge filter type - valid values are: Prewitt, Laplacian, and Sobel. Each represents a different edge detection algorithm. For the ColorAlpha filter type - valid mode values are: equal, lesserthan, and greaterthan. These work in conjunction with SetColorAlphaTarget to define a color target. In lesserthan mode, any values less than the specified target color are made transparent. In greaterthan mode, any values greater than the specified target color are made transparent. In equal mode, any values within the target color and specified tolerance are made transparent. The tolerance is specified by calling SetColorAlphaTolerance. |
| [poIImagePlane SetFilterType](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setfiltertype.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetFilterType ftype` | The image plane offers several image post-processing filters that can be applied on the image or video. This command sets/changes the filter type currently being used on the image plane. | **Inputs:** ftype The filter type name. Valid values are returned by GetFilterTypeList, and consist of, “None”, “Edge”, and “ColorAlpha”. "None" means to use no post processing filter. “Edge” is an edge detection filter. “ColorAlpha” replaces a range of color values in the image with transparency. |
| [poIImagePlane SetFit](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setfit.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetFit val` | This command sets the Fit display option of a 2D image plane. “Fit” means the image plane is resized to fill its window in the graphics area while its aspect ratio is reserved and the entire image plane is within the window. | **Inputs:** val A Boolean value to determine if the Fit display option is applied to the 2D image plane. True indicates that Fit is applied. False indicates that Fit is unchecked. |
| [poIImagePlane SetLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setlabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetLabel` | Each image plane can have a user assigned label, which will also be shown in the HyperView broswer list of image planes. This command sets the label associated with the image plane. | Not stated |
| [poIImagePlane SetMaintainAspectRatio](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setmaintainaspectratio.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetMaintainAspectRatio val` | When image planes are in foreground or background mode, setting this flag causes HyperView to maintain the original image or video’s aspect ratio. Resizing the image plane using the manipulator will continue to maintain the image aspect ration. If the flag is cleared, the image plane can be stretched without regard to aspect ratio. This command controls the value of the flag for the image plane. | **Inputs:** val True or false to maintain the aspect ratio (or not). |
| [poIImagePlane SetMipMapMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setmipmapmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetMipMapMode mode` | Mipmaps are a way to shrink an image while retaining detail, before rending the final image to the display. Some mipmap modes work better than others, depending on the type of image being drawn and how much it is being shrunk. This command sets the current mipmap mode for an image plane. | **Inputs:** mode The mip map mode. Use GetMipMapModeList to get the possible values. “None” specifies no mipmaps are used. Linear1 and Linear2 are variants of linear pixel interpolation. Nearest1 and Nearest2 are variants of nearest pixel selection. |
| [poIImagePlane SetMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetMode mode` | Image planes support three drawing modes: foreground, background, and 3D. In the foreground and background modes, the image or video is drawn as a flat rectangle, either in front of all the 3D model elements in the graphic area, or behind all the 3D model elements in the graphic area, respectively. In 3D mode, the image plane is drawn as a plane in three dimensional model space. It follows the rotation, zoom, and panning of the rest of the model, and can be also be aligned so that the camera angle of the image or video matches the 3D position of the plane relative to the FE model. This command sets/changes the mode that the image plane is in. | **Inputs:** mode The new mode for the image plane. |
| [poIImagePlane SetPosition](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setposition.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetPosition position` | This command positions an image plane within the client window. This call is only valid when the image plane is in foreground or background mode. If the StretchToFit flag is set, positioning has no affect. | **Inputs:** position Indicates the new image position: Center Center of the window. NW or NORTHWEST Upper left corner NE or NORTHEAST Upper right corner SW or SOUTHWEST Lower left corner SE or SOUTHEAST Lower right corner |
| [poIImagePlane SetShowTrackingMarker](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setshowtrackingmarker.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetShowTrackingMarker show` | This command sets the status of the tracking marker when video tracking has been enabled and calculated. | **Inputs:** show Set to True or False to indicate whether or not the video tracker should be drawn. |
| [poIImagePlane SetStretchToFit](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setstretchtofit.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetStretchToFit val` | When an image plane is in background or foreground mode, setting the StretchToFit flag to true will cause the image or video to be stretched to consume the entire HyperMesh window when drawn. This command modifies this flag. | **Inputs:** val True or False to indicate whether to stretch the image (or not). |
| [poIImagePlane SetSyncMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setsyncmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetSyncMode mode` | This command automatically adjusts the image plane video scaling based on the selected mode. | **Inputs:** mode Manual (Default) No changes are made. The synch mode is automatically set to manual if the scaling is changed by the user. Length The scaling is adjusted so that the video and model start and stop animating at the same time. Framerate The scaling is adjusted so that the video and model animate at the same number of frames per second. |
| [poIImagePlane SetSyncModel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setsyncmodel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetSyncModel model` | This command sets the model being synchronized with the image plane. | **Inputs:** model The index of the model to be synchronized. |
| [poIImagePlane SetTrackerColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_settrackercolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetTrackerColor color` | This command sets the RGB values for the color of the video tracking marker. | **Inputs:** color A set of RGB color values that define the color. A string containing three RGB values between 0 and 255 must be passed ("000 000 000"). |
| [poIImagePlane SetTrackingMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_settrackingmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetTrackingMode mode` | This command sets the current tracking mode for the image plane. | **Inputs:** mode None No tracking mode is enabled. Model Enables model tracking. VideoMarker Enables video marker tracking. |
| [poIImagePlane SetUserTransform](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setusertransform.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetUserTransform xformStr` | The image plane maintains a separate user transform to track the position, rotation, and scaling applied to the image plane. This command allows you to set/modify the transform. SetUserTransformList does not alter any data regarding the alignment of a 3D image plane to a model. See GetAlignmentData, LocateBy3Points, and LocateBy2Points for additional information. | **Inputs:** xformStr The new transform data. The format is different, depending on whether the image plane is in 3D mode or foreground/background mode. In 3D mode, the transform string consists of twelve floats, representing the rotation about X, Y, and Z, and the translation in X, Y, and Z. For foreground/background modes, the transform string consists of four floats, representing the X and Y scaling factor followed by the X and Y translation for the image. |
| [poIImagePlane SetVidTrackData](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setvidtrackdata.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetVidTrackData data` | This command explicitly sets the arrays used for video tracking rather than calculating them from a tracking point. This is primarily used for restoring session files. | **Inputs:** data A string in the form “x y X Y X Y X Y…..”, where x and y are floats for the pixel location of the tracking point and the remaining X's and Y's are the offsets per frame order from beginning to end. |
| [poIImagePlane SetVidTrackingWindowSize](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setvidtrackingwindowsize.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetVidTrackingWindowSize size` | This command sets the the size of the window that CalcVidTracking searches after each frame to find the tracking point. | **Inputs:** size An integer value between 5 and 50. |
| [poIImagePlane SetVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setvisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_handle SetVisibility state` | The visibility flag indicates whether the image plane will be drawn or remain invisible. This command alters the visibility. | **Inputs:** state True to make the image plane visible, False to make it invisible. |
| [poIImagePlane SetZoom](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplane_setzoom.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIImageplane_handle SetZoom val` | This command sets the Zoom display option of a 2D image plane to the input value. When the zoom function is active for the image plane, it can be zoomed in/out as 3D models. | **Inputs:** val A Boolean value to determine if the Zoom display option is set to the 2D image plane. When it is true, Zoom is active. When it is false, Zoom is inactive. |
| [poIImagePlaneCtrl AddImagePlane](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplanectrl_addimageplane.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_ctrl_handle AddImagePlane ?label? ?id?` | This command adds/creates a new image plane. The image allows the placing of a graphic image or video into the HyperView model window. | **Inputs:** label Specifies a label to be assigned to the image plane. If not specified, this will be blank. Id Specified a unique ID to be assigned to the image plane. If not specified, a unique ID will be automatically assigned. Upon success, it returns the ID of the newly created image plane. |
| [poIImagePlaneCtrl LowerZOrder](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplanectrl_lowerzorder.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_ctrl_handle LowerZOrder id ?to_bottom?` | Image planes have one of three modes for placement into the client window: foreground, background, or 3D. Background image planes are always drawn behind the model elements. Foreground image planes are always drawn in front of the model elements. When multiple background or foreground image planes are present, the Z order controls how overlapping image planes are drawn with respect to one another. This command moves the specified image plane behind the next image plane that is below it. If the image plane specified is already at the bottom of the Z order list, nothing occurs. If to_bottom is passed as true, the specified image plane is pushed to the bottom of the Z order list. | **Inputs:** id The unique ID of the image plane to modify. ?to_bottom? If “true” is specified, the image plane will be put at the bottom of the z order list. |
| [poIImagePlaneCtrl RaiseZOrder](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplanectrl_raisezorder.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_ctrl_handle RaiseZOrder id ?to_top?` | Image planes have one of three modes for placement into the client window: foreground, background, or 3D. Background image planes are always drawn behind the model elements. Foreground image planes are always drawn in front of the model elements. When multiple background or foreground image planes are present, the Z order controls how overlapping image planes are drawn with respect to one another. This command moves the specified image plane in front of the next image plane that is above it. If the image plane specified is already at the top of the Z order list, nothing occurs. If to_top is passed as true, the specified image plane is pushed to the top of the Z order list. | **Inputs:** id The unique ID of the image plane to modify. ?to_top? If “true” is specified, the image plane will be put at the top of the z order list. |
| [poIImagePlaneCtrl RemoveImagePlane](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplanectrl_removeimageplane.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_ctrl_handle RemoveImagePlane id` | This command removes the specified image plane. | **Inputs:** id The unique ID of the image plane. |
| [poIImagePlaneCtrl SetZOrderList](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiimageplanectrl_setzorderlist.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `Imageplane_ctrl_handle SetZOrderList "idList" type` | Image planes have one of three modes for placement into the client window: foreground, background, or 3D. Background image planes are always drawn behind the model elements. Foreground image planes are always drawn in front of the model elements. When multiple background or foreground image planes are present, the Z order controls how overlapping image planes are drawn with respect to one another. This command defines the order in which image planes are drawn for the specified type. The specified list contains the IDs for the image planes of that type, in the order they will appear in the graphic area. An image listed at the beginning of the list will be drawn in front of image planes at the end of the Z order list. If there are no image planes of the type specified, nothing will be returned by this command. | **Inputs:** idList A list of valid image plane IDs, specified in the order they should appear on the screen. The list must be surrounded by double-quotes. All the IDs in the list must refer to valid image planes configured as 2D. The first ID in the list will be drawn front most, and the last ID in the list will be drawn bottom most. type The type of images to reorder. Valid values are “foreground” and “background”. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl ExportSTL](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_exportstl.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle ExportSTL value` | This command exports an STL format ASCII file. | **Inputs:** value The path of the STL file to be written. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetAverageFactor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setaveragefactor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetAverageFactor` | This command sets the nodal variation factor's valid range.Note: Specify the threshold variation level above which the nodal averaging should not be carried out. | Not stated |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetAverageMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setaveragemode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetAverageMode mode` | This command specifies the method by which elemental results are averaged to nodes. | **Inputs:** mode Values can be: none, simple, maximum, minimum, advanced, difference, Max of corner, Min of corner, or Extreme of corner. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetAvgAcrossPartsEnable](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setavgacrosspartsenable.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetAvgAcrossPartsEnable state` | This command sets the state of the average across parts. | **Inputs:** state Boolean value to set the average across parts state to True or False. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setcolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetColor color` | This command sets the iso surface color value. | **Inputs:** color If the iso value color override is enabled, the iso value geometry will be drawn in this color. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetColorOverride](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setcoloroverride.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetColorOverride override` | This command sets the state of color override option for the iso value. | **Inputs:** override Set to true to change the color, otherwise false. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetCornerDataEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setcornerdataenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetCornerDataEnabled enable` | This command enables node on element data. Note: This state is valid only if the data binding is node-on-element. | **Inputs:** enable Set to true to enable corner data processing, or false to average corner data to the element centroid. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetDataComponent](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setdatacomponent.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetDataComponent dim comp_label` | This command sets the data component for a given component. The component dimension must be enabled before setting. Note: This state is valid only if the data binding is node-on-element. | **Inputs:** dim The component dimension. Options include: "node", "line", "shell", or "solid". comp_label The component label. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetDataType](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setdatatype.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `iso_handle SetDataType data_type_name` | This command sets the data type to be applied for an iso plot (for example, Stress, Velocity, etc.). If the data type name contains spaces, then the name must be specified inside double quotes (for example, "Effective plastic strain"). The data type name must exist in the list of available data types in the results file in order to display a proper contour plot. You can obtain a list of valid data types from the result control by using the GetDataTypeList command. | **Inputs:** data_type_name The name of the data type to be set. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetDimensionEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setdimensionenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetDimensionEnabled dim_label state` | This command sets the dimension state: enabled or disabled. | **Inputs:** dim_label The dimension state label: "line", "shell", or "solid". state True if enabled, false if not. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetDisplayMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setdisplaymode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetDisplayMode set` | This command the current display mode. | **Inputs:** set Set to "equal", "below", or "above". The iso value geometry is drawn based on this option. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetEnabled pool state` | This command sets the dimension state: enabled or disabled. | **Inputs:** pool The pool from which to set the enable state. state True if enabled, or false if not enabled. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetEnableState](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setenablestate.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetEnableState state` | This command the auto load/unload iso value data state.Note: Setting enable state to false causes the currently loaded data to be unloaded. | **Inputs:** state True if the iso value data can be loaded, false if it cannot. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetFeatureAngleAverage](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setfeatureangleaverage.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `iso_handle SetFeatureAngleAverage angle` | This command sets the angle for the feature angle average. The specified angle should be between 0 and 180 degrees. | **Inputs:** angle The angle for the feature angle average. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetFeatureAngleAverageEnableState](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setfeatureangleaverageenablestate.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `iso_handle SetFeatureAngleAverageEnableState state` | This command sets the state of the feature angle average. | **Inputs:** state Boolean value to set the feature angle average state (True or False). |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetIgnoredFlippedNormal](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setignoredflippednormal.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `iso_handle SetIgnoreFlippedNormal state` | This command sets the state of the "Ignore Flipped Normal" option for the feature angle average. | **Inputs:** state A Boolean value to set the "Ignore Flipped Normal" state (True or False). |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetIncrement](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setincrement.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetIncrement increment` | This command sets the current iso value increment. This causes the iso value to shift by this value. | **Inputs:** increment Set to a real value that's less than or equal to the difference between Min (GetIsoValueMin) and Max (GetIsoValueMax). |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetIsoValue](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setisovalue.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetIsoValue value` | This command sets the iso value to the specified value. | **Inputs:** value Set to a float number between Min (GetIsoValueMin) and Max (GetIsoValueMax). |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetLayer](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setlayer.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetLayer layer` | This command sets the current layer.Note: The scalar storage within HyperView can accept a layer or a derived quantity of all layers (see below). | **Inputs:** layer The layer label or one of the following derived types: max Store the maximum value of all layers. min Store the minimum value of all layers. extreme Store the maximum absolute value of all layers. sum Stores the (scalar) sum of all values among the layers for each entity. average Stores the (scalar) average value among the layers which contain data for each entity. range Stores the difference between the maximum and minimum values among the layers for each entity. maxlayer Stores the layer number (instead of the actual value) which contributed to the maximum value for each entity. minlayer Stores the layer number (instead of the actual value) which contributed to the minimum value for each entity. extremelayer Stores the layer number (instead of the actual value) which contributed to the maximum values for each entity. Note: Only in the case of results based at the elemental centroid, would one layer per element be displayed. For corner bound or nodal bound results (in the case of averaging), different layers may contribute to the max/min/extreme value at each corner/node of the same element. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetLegendMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setlegendmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetLegendMode state` | This command sets the legend mode to true or false.Note: If legend mode is set to true, then a contour has to be applied before generating the iso value. In this case, multiple iso values will be generated. | **Inputs:** state Set to true to generate multiple iso values, or false to generate single iso values. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetProjectionRule](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setprojectionrule.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `iso_handle SetProjectionRule rule` | This command retrieves the projection rule to be applied for the 3D tensor, while projecting a 3D tensor to the elemental plane. Possible argument options include: "none" or a string containing two integer values separated by a space (for example, "2 3"). The two numbers that are used represent the primary and secondary axis for projection (1 = x-axis, 2 = y-axis, and 3 = z-axis). | **Inputs:** rule The rule string. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetResultSystem](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setresultsystem.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetResultSystem system_id` | This command sets the result system ID.Note: Setting the result system is valid only for transformable data types, such as vector tensor. | **Inputs:** system_id The result system ID or one of the following identifiers: 0 Global -1 Analysis -2 Elemental -3 Tracking |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetSelectionSet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setselectionset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetSelectionSet set_id` | This command specifies the selected entities to be used by the iso control. | **Inputs:** set_id The set ID. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetShellLayer](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setshelllayer.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIIsoValueCtrl_handle SetShellLayer layer` | This command sets the current layer.Note: The scalar storage within HyperView can accept a shell layer or a derived quantity of all shell layers (max, min, or extreme). | **Inputs:** layer The shell layer label or one of the following derived types: max Stores the maximum value of all layers. min Stores the minimum value of all layers. extreme Stores the maximum absolute values of all layers. |
| [poIIsoValueCtrl SetShowMidsideNodeResults](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiisovaluectrl_setshowmidsidenoderesults.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `iso_handle SetShowMidsideNodeResults state` | This command sets the state of the Show Midside Node Results option for the iso control. | **Inputs:** state Boolean value to set the "Show Midside Node Results" state to true or false. |
| [poILayerFilter AddLayer](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poilayerfilter_addlayer.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poILayerFilter_handle AddLayer layer_name` | This command adds the layer to the layer filter. | **Inputs:** layer_name The name of the layer. |
| [poILayerFilter RemoveAllLayers](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poilayerfilter_removealllayers.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poILayerFilter_handle RemoveAllLayers` | This command removes all layers from the layer filter. | Not stated |
| [poILayerFilter RemoveLayer](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poilayerfilter_removelayer.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poILayerFilter_handle RemoveLayer layer_name` | This command removes the layer from the layer filter. | **Inputs:** layer_name The name of the layer. |
| [poILayerFilter SetDescription](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poilayerfilter_setdescription.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poILayerFilter_handle SetDescription description` | This command sets the description for the layer filter. | **Inputs:** description The description for the layer filter. |
| [poILayerFilter SetLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poilayerfilter_setlabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poILayerFilter_handle SetLabel set_label` | This command sets the label for the layer filter. | **Inputs:** set_label The label for the layer filter. |
| [poILegend SetNumericPrecision](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poilegend_setnumericprecision.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poILegend_handle SetNumericPrecision precision` | This command sets the precision for numbers. | **Inputs:** precision The numeric precision. Maximum is 10. |
| [poILegend SetPosition](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poilegend_setposition.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poILegend_handle SetPosition position` | This command sets the location of the legend. | **Inputs:** position The position of the legend: upper left, upper right, lower left, lower right. |
| [poILegend SetReverseEnable](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poilegend_setreverseenable.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poILegend_handle SetReverseEnable enable` | This command enables/disables the reverse state of the legend. | **Inputs:** enable True or false. |
| [poILegend SetShowCategoryID](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poilegend_setshowcategoryid.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `legend_handle SetShowCategoryID state` | This command sets the state of the Show Category ID option for the Category Legend. | **Inputs:** state Boolean value to set the Show Category ID option to true or false. If the state is to set to true, the Category ID will be shown in the Category Legend. If the state is set to false, the Category labels will be shown in the Category Legend. |
| [poILegend SetStyle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poilegend_setstyle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poILegend_handle SetStyle state` | This command sets the legend style. | **Inputs:** state The legend style. |
| [poILegend SetType](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poilegend_settype.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poILegend_handle SetType type` | This command sets the legend type. | **Inputs:** type The legend type: static, dynamic, or user-defined. |
| [poILegend SetVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poilegend_setvisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poILegend_handle SetVisibility state` | This command sets the legend visibility. | **Inputs:** state True If visible False If not visible |
| [poILegend SyncWithClone](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poilegend_syncwithclone.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poILegend_handle SyncWithClone` | This command applies the settings for the cloned legend to the original legend. | Not stated |
| [poILegend UnOverrideValue](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poilegend_unoverridevalue.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poILegend_handle UnOverrideValue value_idx` | This command removes the override value for the given color level. | **Inputs:** value_idx The level index. |
| [poIMeasure AddDynamicCurveToPlot](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_adddynamiccurvetoplot.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle AddDynamicCurveToPlot item_idx y_axis plotwin` | This command adds a dynamic curve for this measure, using the specified y-axis, to the given plot client. | **Inputs:** item_idx Measure item index (zero based). y_axis Measure option mapped to the plot's y-axis. Options include: mag, x_comp, y_comp, z_comp, and scalar. plotwin Plot window handle to which to add a curve. |
| [poIMeasure AddDynamicXYCurveToPlot](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_adddynamicxycurvetoplot.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle AddDynamicXYCurveToPlot x_axis y_axis plotwin` | This command adds a dynamic curve for all entity IDs in the current measure group, using the specified y-axis, to the given plot client. | **Inputs:** x_axis The attributes of the group of measured entities. Options include: entity-id, entity-x, entity-y, entity-z, and distance. y_axis Measure option mapped to the plot's y-axis. Options include: mag, x_comp, y_comp, z_comp, and scalar. plotwin Plot window handle to which to add a curve. |
| [poIMeasure AddElement](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_addelement.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle AddElement elem_id` | This command adds an element to the measure. | **Inputs:** elem_id The model id, element id, and the optional element dimension. Or The model id, element dimension, and element id (order dependent). |
| [poIMeasure AddEntity](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_addentity.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle AddEntity id_str` | This command adds the given entity ID/s in the current measure group from the specified model and pool. | **Inputs:** id_str Can be one of the following formats: Regular Nodes "node model_id [node_pool] n1" Param Nodes "node model_id [node_pool] n1 n2 %1" Param-Param Node "node model_id [node_pool] n1 n2 %1 n3 n4 %2 %3" Elements "element model_id [element_pool] element_id" Components "part model_id [part_pool] part_id" Systems "system model_id [system_pool] system_id" |
| [poIMeasure AddMinDistFromSet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_addmindistfromset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle AddMinDistFromSet set_id_str` | This command will supply the measure object with the selection set containing the entities (components/elements/nodes) that will be used as the “from” side for minimum distance computation. | **Inputs:** set_id_str The identifier for the selection set. |
| [poIMeasure AddMinDistToSet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_addmindisttoset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle AddMinDistToSet set_id_str` | This command will supply the measure object with the selection set containing the entities (components/elements/nodes) that will be used as the “to” side for minimum distance computation. | **Inputs:** set_id_str The identifier for the selection set. |
| [poIMeasure AddNode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_addnode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle AddNode "model_id node_id"` | This command adds the given node of the current/specified model to the measure. Note: HyperView allows you to specify the measure between several models loaded (overlaid) into the same window. This is why you have to provide the model ID and node ID (or element ID) when you add a node or element to the measure. | **Inputs:** node_id The node ID in one of three forms: "m1 n1" (A regular node) This option adds a node from a model with the ID = m1 and the node ID = n1. This is the most common function you will use to create measures from scripts. The following two options are used mostly by the HyperView GUI to create a measure on a section cut, or Iso surface. "m1 n1 n2 %1" (A param node) This option adds a "parametric" node from the model with the ID = m1 and the nodes n1 and n2, and at %1 distance between n1 and n2. "m1 n1 n2 %1 n3 n4 %2 %3" (A param-param node) This option adds a "param-param" node from the following model types: A model with the ID = m1 and the nodes n1 and n2 and at %1 distance between n1 & n2. A model with the ID = m2 and the nodes n3 and n4 and at %2 distance between n3 & n4, and at %3 distance between (m1, n1, n2, %1) and (m2, n3, n4, %2). |
| [poIMeasure AddStaticCurveToPlot](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_addstaticcurvetoplot.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle AddStaticCurveToPlot item_idx y_axis plotwin` | This command adds a static curve for this measure, using the specified y-axis, to the given plot client. | **Inputs:** item_idx Measure item index (zero based). y_axis Measure option mapped to the plot's y-axis. Options include: mag, x_coord, y_coord, z_coord, and scalar. plotwin Plot window handle to which to add a curve. |
| [poIMeasure AddStaticXYCurveToPlot](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_addstaticxycurvetoplot.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle AddStaticXYCurveToPlot x_axis y_axis plotwin` | This command adds a static curve for all entity IDs in the current measure group, using the specified y-axis, to the given plot client. | **Inputs:** x_axis The attributes of the group of measured entities. Options include: entity-id, entity-x, entity-y, entity-z, and distance. y_axis Measure option mapped to the plot's y-axis. Options include: mag, x_comp, y_comp, z_comp, and scalar. plotwin Plot window handle to which to add a curve. |
| [poIMeasure AddSystem](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_addsystem.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle AddSystem marker_id_str` | This command adds a marker to the measure. | **Inputs:** marker_id_str The model marker ID. |
| [poIMeasure CreateItem](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_createitem.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle CreateItem save_from_set save_to_set` | This command will create the measure item. | **Inputs:** save_from_set (Optional) Boolean argument to save the "from" set. save_to_set (Optional) Boolean argument to save the "to" set. |
| [poIMeasure HighlightEntitySet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_highlightentityset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle HighlightEntitySet item_index` | This command will will highlight the entity sets for the particular measure item. The “from” selection will be highlighted in green and the “to” selection will be highlighted in white.Note: The Review mode for the measure group must be set to “true”. | **Inputs:** item_index The index of the measure item within the group. |
| [poIMeasure RemoveItem](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_removeitem.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle RemoveItem idx` | This command removes the given measure item. | Not stated |
| [poIMeasure SetBaseTime](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_setbasetime.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle SetBaseTime sec` | This command sets the measure base time (for relative measures only). | **Inputs:** sec The base time. |
| [poIMeasure SetColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_setcolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle SetColor color` | This command sets the measure text color. | **Inputs:** color The note text color. |
| [poIMeasure SetDisplayMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_setdisplaymode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle SetDisplayMode name state` | This command sets the measure display mode. | **Inputs:** name The display option name. Options include: label, id, project, mag, x_comp, y_comp, z_comp, scalar, system, min, max, node_path, distance, transparency, autohide, and prefix. state The display option value: true for on, false for off. |
| [poIMeasure SetLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_setlabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle SetLabel label` | This command sets the measure label. | **Inputs:** label The measure label. |
| [poIMeasure SetMagnitudeDef](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_setmagnitudedef.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle SetMagnitudeDef definition` | This command sets the measure magnitude definition. Only valid for a distance type measure resolved in a rectangular system (including Relative Displacement, Distance Between, Incremental Distance, Minimum Distance). Measure magnitude definition includes: X+Y+Z: xyz norm = sqrt(x^2+y^2+z^2) X+Y: x-y norm = sqrt(x^2+y^2) X+Z: x-z norm = sqrt(x^2+ z^2) Y+Z: y-z norm = sqrt(y^2+z^2) None: Do not show magnitude and define as X+Y+Z | **Inputs:** definition The measure definition. Options include: X+Y+Z, X+Y, X+Z, Y+Z, and None. |
| [poIMeasure SetResultSystem](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_setresultsystem.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle SetResultSystem sys_id` | This command sets the measure analysis system. | **Inputs:** sys_id The system ID in form: 'model_id system_id', you can omit model_id - current model will be used to use tracking system specify -3. |
| [poIMeasure SetReviewMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_setreviewmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle SetReviewMode item_index` | This command will set the review mode for the measure group. | **Inputs:** item_index The index of the measure item within the group. |
| [poIMeasure SetType](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_settype.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle SetType` | This command sets the measure type. Measure types include: Position, Relative Displacement, Distance Between, Incremental Distance, Minimum Distance, Relative Angle, Angle Between, Incremental Angle, Static MinMax Result, Dynamic MinMax Result, Nodal Contour, Elemental Contour, Component Contour, Node Path, and Yaw Pitch Roll. | Not stated |
| [poIMeasure SetVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_setvisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle SetVisibility state` | This command sets the measure visibility. | **Inputs:** state True The measure is visible. False The measure is not visible. |
| [poIMeasure UnlinkItem](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimeasure_unlinkitem.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIMeasure_handle UnlinkItem` | This command unlinks the live connection between this measure item and the curve to which it's connected. | Not stated |
| [poIModel AddAssembly](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_addassembly.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle AddAssembly assy_id label parent_assy_loc` | This command creates a new assembly. | **Inputs:** assy_id The assembly ID. label The assembly label. parent_assy_loc The parent assembly locator string used by the newly created assembly. |
| [poIModel AddCrossSection](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_addcrosssection.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle AddCrossSection type id` | This command adds a new cross section of the specified type. | **Inputs:** type (string) Specify the section type: planar or set. id (int) The ID of the cross section. |
| [poIModel AddExplosion](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_addexplosion.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle AddExplosion` | This command adds a new explosion to the current model. | Not stated |
| [poIModel AddPart](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_addpart.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle AddPart part_handle assy_locator relative_to` | This command creates a part from the given part reference parented by the given assembly locator string. | **Inputs:** part_handle The part handle from to create a reference. assy_locator The assembly locator string. |
| [poIModel AddSelectionSet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_addselectionset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `model_handle AddSelectionSet type` | This command creates a new selection set of a particular type on the current model. Using the ID that is returned by this command, you can retrieve the handle to the newly created selection set and perform actions such as adding entities and setting the visibility on it. | **Inputs:** type Type can be either node, element, part, system, or assembly. |
| [poIModel AddTrackingSystem](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_addtrackingsystem.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle AddTrackingSystem` | This command creates a tracking system and assigns a name. | Not stated |
| [poIModel AddUserDefinedSystem](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_adduserdefinedsystem.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `model_handle AddUserDefinedSystem system_type` | This command adds a new coordinate system to the current model. The following types of systems can be created: "Rectangular", "Cylindrical", or "Spherical". | **Inputs:** system_type The system type - which can be any of the following values: "Rectangular", "Cylindrical", or "Spherical". |
| [poIModel ApplyDefaults](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_applydefaults.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle ApplyDefaults` | This command sets component material to default values. Calling this command on an assembly has no affect. | Not stated |
| [poIModel ApplyStyleTo](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_applystyle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle ApplyStyleTo destination style` | This command applies the style of the current model to another model on the current page, or on a different page in the session. In the case of applying a style to more than one model, use this command multiple times. | **Inputs:** destination The model to which you want to apply the styles of the current model. style The style(s) attributes you want to apply to another model. Supported style attributes include: Entity Attributes Collision Contour Deformed Shape Explosion Set Iso Value Legend Result Steps Tensor Plot Tracing Tracking Vector Plot Visibility Symmetry System Plot System FBD Plot Model Attributes |
| [poIModel ChangeSelectionSetType](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_changeselectionsettype.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `model_handle ChangeSelectionSetType id type` | This command changes the type of the selection set and takes care of the entity binding. The type is determined when creating a new selection set using the command poIModel AddSelectionSet. If the type needs to be changed, all the entities need to change, too. Type can either be node, element, part, system or assembly. Id is the current selection set ID. | **Inputs:** id The ID of the selection set on which you want to perform a type change. type The type to which the selection set should be changed. |
| [poIModel DefineAssembly](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_defineassembly.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle DefineAssembly assemblyID label parentAssemblyID` | This command defines an assembly in a model. | **Inputs:** assemblyID The ID of the new assembly to be defined. label The name of the new assembly. parentAssemblyID The ID of the parent assembly (already existing in the model). |
| [poIModel Mask](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_mask.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle Mask set_id` | This command masks the contents of a selection set. | **Inputs:** set_id The selection set index. |
| [poIModel MaskAll](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_maskall.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle MaskAll pool_type` | This command masks all parts or systems. | **Inputs:** pool_type The pool type: element, part, or system. |
| [poIModel MaskOffScreen](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_maskoffscreen.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle MaskOffScreen` | This command masks off screen elements. | Not stated |
| [poIModel RemoveAssembly](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_removeassembly.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle RemoveAssembly assemblyID` | This command removes an assembly from the model. | **Inputs:** assemblyID The ID of the assembly to be removed from the model. |
| [poIModel RemoveExplosion](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_removeexplosion.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle RemoveExplosion id` | This command removes the given explosion by ID. | **Inputs:** id The explosion ID. |
| [poIModel RemovePart](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_removepart.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle RemovePart part_locator` | This command removes the given part reference from its parent. | **Inputs:** part_locator The string containing the part locator. |
| [poIModel RemoveResult](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_removeresult.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle RemoveResult` | This command removes a result from the model. | Not stated |
| [poIModel RemoveSelectionSet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_removeselectionset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `model_handle RemoveSelectionSet id` | This command removes a particular selection set defined by the argument id from the current model. | **Inputs:** id The selection set you want to remove. |
| [poIModel RemoveTrackingSystem](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_removetrackingsystem.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle RemoveTrackingSystem name` | This command removes the given tracking system by ID. | **Inputs:** name The tracking system ID. |
| [poIModel RemoveUserDefinedSystem](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_removeuserdefinedsystem.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `model_handle RemoveUserDefinedSystem id_str` | This command removes a coordinate system from the current model. Only user-defined systems can be deleted using this command. Systems that are part of the input deck cannot be deleted. | **Inputs:** id_str The ID of the user-defined system. |
| [poIModel ReverseMask](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_reversemask.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle ReverseMask pool_type` | This command reverses the visibility on elements and systems. | **Inputs:** pool_type The pool type: element, part, or system. |
| [poIModel SetActiveExplosion](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_setactiveexplosion.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle SetActiveExplosion id` | This command sets the active explosion by ID. | **Inputs:** id The ID of the explosion to be activated. |
| [poIModel SetActiveTrackingSystem](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_setactivetrackingsystem.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle SetActiveTrackingSystem id` | This command sets the active tracking system by ID. | **Inputs:** id The tracking system ID. |
| [poIModel SetApproximationError](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_setapproximationerror.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle SetApproximationError error` | This command sets the model's approximation error. | **Inputs:** error ASCII percent error for data. |
| [poIModel SetAssemblyID](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_setassemblyid.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle SetAssemblyID assy_id` | This command sets the parent to the assembly identified by assy_id. This will always fail. You cannot change the model's parent. | **Inputs:** assy_id The assembly ID to move this part/assembly into. |
| [poIModel SetAttribute](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_setattribute.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle SetAttribute` | This command enables or disables the selection set identified by the set_id. | Not stated |
| [poIModel SetColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_setcolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle SetColor str` | This command sets the component's color index. This command will be invalid if it is called on a component that is an assembly, doing so will have no affect, and a warning will be returned. | **Inputs:** str The color identifier string. |
| [poIModel SetColorMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_setcolormode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle SetColorMode mode` | This command sets the current color mode setting of the model. | **Inputs:** mode Options include: component, model, normal, or partassembly. |
| [poIModel SetDisplayMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_setdisplaymode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle SetDisplayMode name state` | This command sets the component display option setting. If this component is an assembly, all the child parts will be set to this state. | **Inputs:** name Displays option name: "id", "boundingbox", or "infit". state Displays option value: "true" if the given display option is on, or "false" if the given display option is off. |
| [poIModel SetLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_setlabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle SetLabel name` | This command sets the model label. | **Inputs:** name The ASCII label. |
| [poIModel SetMaterial](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_setmaterial.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle SetMaterial id` | This command sets the component material index. Calling this command on an assembly has no affect. | **Inputs:** id The material ID to assign to this component. |
| [poIModel SetMeshMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_setmeshmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle SetMeshMode mode` | This command sets the component's mesh mode. If this is an assembly, ALL child parts are set to the specified mesh mode. | **Inputs:** mode The mesh mode: edges, features, meshlines, or none. |
| [poIModel SetPolygonMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_setpolygonmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle SetPolygonMode mode` | This command sets the component's polygon mode. If this is an assembly, ALL child parts are set to the specified polygon mode. | **Inputs:** mode The polygon mode: transparent, opaque, or wireframe. |
| [poIModel SetSync](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_setsync.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle SetSync` | This command sets the model sync value. | Not stated |
| [poIModel SetTracingMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_settracingmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle SetTracingMode mode` | This command sets the component's tracing mode. This command has no affect on an assembly. | **Inputs:** mode The tracing mode: off, first, all, or last (# of steps). |
| [poIModel SetTrackingSystemDisplayMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_settrackingsystemdisplaymode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle SetTrackingSystemDisplayMode` | This command sets the tracking system drawing mode. | Not stated |
| [poIModel SetTranslation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_settranslation.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle SetTranslation translation_vector` | This command sets the translation vector in the global coordinate system. If this component is an assembly, a zero translation will always be returned. Translating/exploding the root level (model) assembly is an invalid operation. The translation will be put into a TRANSLATION mode explosion. | **Inputs:** translation_vector The translation vector. |
| [poIModel SetVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_setvisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle SetVisibility state` | This command sets the model's visibility state (doesn't affect part/element visibility). | **Inputs:** state True if the model is visible, or false if the model is not visible. |
| [poIModel UnMask](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_unmask.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle UnMask set_id` | This command unmasks the contents of a selection set. | **Inputs:** set_id The selection set index. |
| [poIModel UnMaskAll](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_unmaskall.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle UnMaskAll` | This command unmasks all elements. | Not stated |
| [poIModel UnMaskOnScreen](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poimodel_unmaskonscreen.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIModel_handle UnMaskOnScreen` | This command unmasks screen elements. | Not stated |
| [poINote GetTextColorMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_gettextcolormode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle GetTextColorMode` | This command returns the color mode for the text of the note. | Not stated |
| [poINote SetAlignment](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setalignment.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetAlignment alignment` | This command sets the text alignment for the note. | **Inputs:** alignment The vector type. Options include: "left", "right", or "center". |
| [poINote SetAttachment](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setattachment.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetAttachment "attachment"` | This command sets the note attachment method. | **Inputs:** "attachment" The attachment method. Options include: window The note remains stationary when the animation image is repositioned in the window. "node model_id node_id" The note can be anchored to any node in the model. Note attached to model = model_id. Node with id = node_id. "element model_id element_pool element_id" The note can be anchored to any element in the model. Note attached to a model = model_id. Element pool = element_pool. Element with id = element_id. If the element pool is not specified, the selection will be done from the user pool as default. coord The note can be anchored to the XYZ coordinates of the model. |
| [poINote SetAutoHide](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setautohide.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetAutoHide state` | This command sets the note auto-hide attribute. | **Inputs:** state True Hides the notes if the leader is occluded. False Disables this feature. |
| [poINote SetBackgroundColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setbackgroundcolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `note_handle SetBackgroundColor` | This command sets the background color, or the fill color, for the note. This command will not have any effect if the transparency option is "on" for that note. | Not stated |
| [poINote SetBackgroundColorMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setbackgroundcolormode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetBackgroundColorMode mode` | This command sets the color mode for the background fill of the note. | **Inputs:** mode The color mode to be set. The following options are available: "User" Allows you to specify a user defined color using the poINote SetColor command. "Component" The color to which the entity is attached will be used (if available), otherwise the default color will be used. "Contour" The contour color of the entity to which the note is attached will be used. If no contour is available, gray will be used. |
| [poINote SetBorderThickness](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setborderthickness.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetBorderThickness border_thickness` | This command sets the note border thickness. | **Inputs:** border_thickness The border thickness. |
| [poINote SetBorderThickness](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setborderthickness_2.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetBorderThickness border_thickness` | This command sets the note border thickness. | **Inputs:** border_thickness The border thickness. |
| [poINote SetColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setcolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetColor color` | This command sets the note border and leader line color. | **Inputs:** color The note border and leader line color. |
| [poINote SetColorMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setcolormode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetColorMode mode` | This command sets the color mode for the border of the note. | **Inputs:** mode The color mode to be set. The following options are available: "User" Allows you to specify a user defined color using the poINote SetColor command. "Component" The color to which the entity is attached will be used (if available), otherwise the default color will be used. "Contour" The contour color of the entity to which the note is attached will be used. If no contour is available, gray will be used. |
| [poINote SetFont](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setfont.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetFont font_name` | This command sets the font by name. | **Inputs:** font_name The name of the font. |
| [poINote SetLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setlabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetLabel name` | This command sets the note label. | Not stated |
| [poINote SetName](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setname.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetName note` | This command sets the note label. | Not stated |
| [poINote SetPosition](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setposition.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetPosition location poINote_handle SetPosition {x_pos y_pos} poINote_handle SetPosition {x_pos y_pos z_pos}` | This command sets the note position. | **Inputs:** This command has two possible argument types: Two value list: used with the "screen" position type (see poINote::SetPositionType) x_pos: The x coordinate on the screen; value between 0 (left) and 1 (right). y_pos: The y coordinate on the screen; value between 0 (top) and 1 (bottom). Three value list: used with the “coordinate” position type (see poINote::SetPositionType) x_pos: The x coordinate in model coordinates. y_pos: The y coordinate in model coordinates. z_pos: The z coordinate in model coordinates. |
| [poINote SetPositionToAttachment](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setpositiontoattachment.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `note_handle SetPositionToAttachment true/false` | If “true” is passed to this command, the lower left corner of the bounding box of the note will be positioned at the attachment point. The attachment location could be on the node, the element centroid, or the part centroid - depending upon the entity type to which the note is attached. | Not stated |
| [poINote SetPositionType](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setpositiontype.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetPositionType type` | This command sets the position type of the note. | **Inputs:** type The position type to be set. The following options are available: "screen" This option sets the note to use screen coordinates (X and Y location on the screen). "coordinates" This option sets the note to use 3D coordinates (X, Y, and Z in model coordinates). |
| [poINote SetRotationAngle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setrotationangle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetRotationAngle angle` | This command sets the rotation angle of the note. | **Inputs:** angle The rotation angle of the note in degrees (rotation is applied in a counterclockwise direction). |
| [poINote SetScreenAnchor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setanchortoscreen.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `note_handle SetScreenAnchor true/false` | If a note is anchored, it will remain stationary when the model is panned/rotated, and the leader line is either stretched or shrunk so that the note will maintain in its current position. | Not stated |
| [poINote SetShape](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setshape.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `note_handle SetShape shape` | This command sets the note shape. | **Inputs:** shape The shape of the note: Rectangle, Circle, or Ellipse. |
| [poINote SetText](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_settext.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetText note` | This command sets the note text. | **Inputs:** note The note text. |
| [poINote SetTextColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_settextcolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetTextColor color` | This command sets the note text color. | **Inputs:** color The note text color. |
| [poINote SetTextColorMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_settextcolormode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetTextColorMode mode` | This command sets the text color mode for the note. | **Inputs:** mode The color mode to be set. The following options are available: "Contrast" Maintains the contrast between the text and the background color. "User" Allows you to specify a user defined color using the poINote SetColor command. "Component" The color to which the entity is attached will be used (if available), otherwise the default color will be used. "Contour" The contour color of the entity to which the note is attached will be used. If no contour is available, gray will be used. |
| [poINote SetTransparency](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_settransparency.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetTransparency value` | This command sets the transparency attribute value (true/false). | **Inputs:** value True Sets the transparency. False Does not set the transparency. |
| [poINote SetVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poinote_setvisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poINote_handle SetVisibility state` | This command sets the note visibility. | **Inputs:** state True The note is visible. False The note is not visible. |
| [poIPart ApplyDefaults](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_applydefaults.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle ApplyDefaults` | This command sets the part material to default values. | Not stated |
| [poIPart Rotate](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_rotate.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle Rotate` | This command rotates the part about the axis and angle. | Not stated |
| [poIPart SetAutoColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_setautocolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetAutoColor color_str` | This command sets the part's auto color index. | **Inputs:** color_str The color index or space separated RGB string. |
| [poIPart SetColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_setcolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetColor str` | This command sets the part's color index. | **Inputs:** str The color identifier string. |
| [poIPart SetDefaultColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_setdefaultcolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetDefaultColor str` | This command sets the part default color index. Generally, only the standard color commands should be used. The default color commands are used by the model reading code to control what was used as the default color (as opposed to the user set color). | **Inputs:** str The color identifier string. |
| [poIPart SetDisplayMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_setdisplaymode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetDisplayMode name state` | This command sets the part's display mode. | **Inputs:** name The display option name: Visibility Features Edges Blend Polygon ID Boundingbox Highlight Infit Incut Iniso Meshlines state The display option value: true (on) or false (off). |
| [poIPart SetEulerAngles](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_seteulerangles.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetEulerAngles str` | This command sets the Euler angles (Z-X-Z) with respect to the system defined by the call to poIModel SetRelativeTo. | **Inputs:** str The color identifier string. |
| [poIPart SetIDVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_setidvisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetIDVisibility state` | This command sets the part's ID visibility. | **Inputs:** state The display option value: true (on) or false (off). |
| [poIPart SetLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_setlabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetLabel name` | This command sets the part's label. | **Inputs:** name The ASCII label. |
| [poIPart SetMaterial](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_setmaterial.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetMaterial id` | This command sets the part's material index. | **Inputs:** id The material ID assigned to the part. |
| [poIPart SetMeshMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_setmeshmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetMeshMode mode` | This command sets the part's mesh mode. | **Inputs:** mode The mesh mode: edges, features, meshlines, or none. |
| [poIPart SetOrientation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_setorientation.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetOrientation str` | This command sets the part's orientation in the local coordinate system by the given Euler angles ZXZ. | **Inputs:** str The color identifier string. |
| [poIPart SetOrigin](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_setorigin.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetOrigin str` | This command sets the part origin in the local coordinate system by the given vector. | **Inputs:** str The new XYZ origin of the added object. |
| [poIPart SetPolygonMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_setpolygonmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetPolygonMode mode` | This command sets the part's polygon mode. | **Inputs:** mode Transparent, opaque, or wireframe. |
| [poIPart SetPosition](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_setposition.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetPosition str` | This command sets the part position in the local coordinate system by the given vector. | **Inputs:** str The color identifier string. |
| [poIPart SetRelativeTo](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_setrelativeto.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetRelativeTo id_string` | This command sets positions relative to the parent assembly, local, or global. | **Inputs:** id_string Global, local, or a marker ID string that belongs to the same assembly as the part. |
| [poIPart SetScale](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_setscale.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetScale` | This command sets the part scale factors for each axis. | Not stated |
| [poIPart SetShadeMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_setshademode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetShadeMode mode` | This command sets the shading mode.
