This command overrides the global setting that was set with poIRenderOptions SetShadeMode. | **Inputs:** mode Set to flat or smooth. |
| [poIPart SetTracingMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_settracingmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetTracingMode mode` | This command sets the part's tracing mode. | **Inputs:** mode The tracing mode: off, first, all, or last number of steps. |
| [poIPart SetVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipart_setvisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPart_handle SetVisibility state` | This command sets the part's visibility. Note: Using this command in a loop to hide multiple components, may result in slower perforce. To improve performance, it is recommended that the Mask command be used (in the model handle) with a set ID. See the poIModel Mask topic for additional details and examples. | **Inputs:** state True if visible, or false if not. |
| [poIPartAssembly SetVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipartassembly_setvisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPartAssembly_handle SetVisibility state` | This command sets the visibility of the part assembly. Setting the visibility of the part assembly will also affect the visibility of all child part assemblies and components within the part assembly hierarchy. | **Inputs:** mode Show/Hide the part assembly (true/false). |
| [poIPartSet SetVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipartset_setvisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPartSet_handle SetVisibility state` | This command sets the visibility of the part set. Setting the visibility of the part set will also affect the visibility of all child part set and part assemblies within the part set hierarchy. | **Inputs:** state Show/Hide the part set (true/false). |
| [poIPost AddBestView](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_addbestview.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `post_handle AddBestView` | This command adds/creates a BestView entry that can show a given entity ID. | Not stated |
| [poIPost AddGraphicMaterial](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_addgraphicmaterial.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle AddGraphicMaterial` | This command adds a material to the database.Note: Adds a material that describes the graphical properties of a surface, such as shininess, emissiveness, and specular color. | Not stated |
| [poIPost AddMeasure](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_addmeasure.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle AddMeasure type` | This command creates a measure. | **Inputs:** type The type of the measure. If not specified, an undefined measure is created. Valid values are: Angle Between, Circle Radius, Distance Between, Elemental Contour, Incremental Angle, Incremental Distance, Minimum Distance, Nodal Contour, Node Path, Position, Relative Angle, Relative Displacement, and Yaw Pitch Roll. |
| [poIPost AddModel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_addmodel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle AddModel filename reader_name` | This command loads the given model file, overlaying any existing models.Note: See RemoveAllModels() before loading if overlaying is not desired. | **Inputs:** filename The model file name. reader_name (Optional) The model reader name. Use GetModelReaderList to retrieve a list of all the available model reader names. |
| [poIPost AddModelToResultOverlay](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_addmodeltoresultoverlay.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle AddModelToResultOverlay hwID` | This command adds the model from participate in result overlay. For these models, the plot will be shown when contour, tensor, vector or iso is applied. | **Inputs:** hwID The ID of the model to be added in result overlay. |
| [poIPost AddNote](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_addnote.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle AddNote` | This command adds a note. | Not stated |
| [poIPost AddQueryRule](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_addqueryrule.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `client_handle AddQueryRule rule` | This command adds the query rule to the advanced query. | **Inputs:** rule Accepts only the following: expression, extreme, mindist, ignoreelemtype, and selectionset. |
| [poIPost AddResult](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_addresult.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `post_handle AddResult label context notify` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, adds a result definition with the given label. It also returns the ID of the created result definition. | **Inputs:** label The result definition label. context A string indicating the context of the result ID (scalar, tensor, vector, iso). notify Set to 'true' to trigger an event to update the browser, otherwise 'false'. The default value is true. |
| [poIPost AddResultMathAnalysis](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_addresultmathanalysis.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle AddResultMathAnalysis tmpl mfile rfile mreader rreader add_resfiles` | This command adds a model and result pair to HyperView and enables result math. The result math operations which are available will depend on the template name specified. The Model reader name and Result reader name arguments are optional.Note: If the AddModel and SetResult commands are used, result math will not be enabled. | **Inputs:** tmpl The result module template name (for example, Standard, Advanced, NVH). mfile The path of the model file. rfile The path of the result file. mreader (Optional) Specifies the reader name which will be used to read the model file. rreader (Optional) Specifies the reader name which will be used to read the result file. add_resfiles (Optional) Specifies the path of additional result files to be loaded along with the model file and result file. The additional result files are separated by a semicolon (;). |
| [poIPost AddSection](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_addsection.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle AddSection type_args` | This command adds a new section of the specified type to the current client. | **Inputs:** type_args Specify the section type between planar and spherical.0: planar 1: spherical Note: Section handle commands for planar and spherical sections are under poISection and poISphericalSection respectively. |
| [poIPost ApplyStyleTo](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_applystyleto.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle ApplyStyleTo destination style` | This command applies the style of the current model to another model on the current page, or on a different page in the session. Use this command multiple times to apply a style to more than one target window or model. | **Inputs:** destination The HyperView window or model to which you want to apply the styles of the current model. style The style(s) attributes you want to apply to another model. Supported style attributes include: Boundary Geometry Measures Notes Section Cut Model Info User Views View Orientation Image Planes |
| [poIPost ArrangeNotes](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_arrangenotes.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `post_handle ArrangeNotes option` | This command arranges the given notes around the screen such that they are close to the attachment point, but moved off of the model and legend areas. | **Inputs:** option This can be either "all" or a list of note IDs to be arranged. "all": All displayed notes will be arranged. List of IDs: Only the notes with the specified IDs will be arranged.Note: The API will not place the model note, even if specified. |
| [poIPost CancelLoading](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_cancelloading.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle CancelLoading notify` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, cancels all loading operations that are currently in progress. This includes both simulation step and result loading operations. Returns Success (0) if cancelling was successful or Warning (2) if there was a problem during cancelling. | **Inputs:** notify Set to 'true' to trigger an event to update the browser, otherwise 'false'. The default value is true. |
| [poIPost CaptureImage](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_captureimage.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle CaptureImage filename format quality` | This command renders the current HyperView window to an off-screen image file. | **Inputs:** filename The name of the file. format JPEG (default) or PNG. quality The image quality (1-4, with 1 as the default). |
| [poIPost CaptureImageByRegion](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_captureimagebyregion.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle CaptureImageByRegion regionType filename quality` | This command renders the current HyperView window, crops the rendering to the specified region, and then writes it to an off-screen image. | **Inputs:** regionType The region of the window which will be saved. MODELSONLY specifies that the window will be cropped so that only the models will be visible. filename The name of the file. quality The image quality (1-4, with 1 as the default). |
| [poIPost Clear](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_clear.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle Clear` | This command resets the current post instance by removing all models and resetting the view (as well as any other post state). | Not stated |
| [poIPost ClearBestViewNotes](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_clearbestviewnotes.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `post_handle ClearBestViewNotes` | This command clears the notes on all of the best views. | Not stated |
| [poIPost CopyResult](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_copyresult.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `post_handle CopyResult id context notify` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, copies a result definition from another definition. It also returns the ID of the created result definition. | **Inputs:** id The result definition label ID to copy from. context A string indicating the context of the result to copy (scalar, tensor, vector, iso). notify Set to 'true' to trigger an event to update the browser, otherwise 'false'. The default value is true. |
| [poIPost Draw](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_draw.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle Draw` | This command redraws the current window. | Not stated |
| [poIPost ExportH3D](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_exporth3d.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle ExportH3D filename options` | This command exports an H3D format file. | **Inputs:** filename The complete path of the H3D file to be written. options (Optional) The options available when exporting an H3D file. Preface the option with "no" to toggle the default. Options include: allmodels (default: false) entityattrs (default: true) exportvisible (default: true) animation (default: true) entitysets (default: true) results (default: true) viewattrs (default: true) html (default: true) preview (default: true) adapt (default: false) erosion (default: false) compress (default: false) float prevheight (default: 0) int >=0 prevwidth (default: 0) int >=0 prevscalefactor (default: 0) float Note: All options must be specified in quotes (""). |
| [poIPost ExportResultDefinitions](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_exportresultdefinitions.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `post_handle ExportResultDefinitions filename` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, exports the result definitions to an XML file. | **Inputs:** filename The name of the XML to which the definitions will be exported. |
| [poIPost HideAllSections](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_hideallsections.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle HideAllSections` | This command hides all sections available in the window. | Not stated |
| [poIPost ImportResultDefinitions](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_importresultdefinitions.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `post_handle ImportResultDefinitions filename` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, imports the result definitions from an XML file. Returns Success (0) or Warning (2) if the file does not exist. | **Inputs:** filename The name of the XML from which the definitions will be imported. |
| [poIPost RemoveAdvancedQuery](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_removeadvancedquery.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `client_handle RemoveAdvancedQuery id` | This command removes the advanced query with a specified ID. | **Inputs:** id Accepts a query ID which is an integer. |
| [poIPost RemoveAllModels](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_removeallmodels.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle RemoveAllModels` | This command removes all loaded models. | Not stated |
| [poIPost RemoveBestView](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_removebestview.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `client_handle RemoveBestView id` | This command removes the BestView with a specified ID. | **Inputs:** id The ID of the BestView already existing in the post handle. |
| [poIPost RemoveGraphicMaterial](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_removegraphicmaterial.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle RemoveGraphicMaterial id` | This command removes the graphical material from the database. | **Inputs:** id The graphic material ID to remove. |
| [poIPost RemoveMeasure](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_removemeasure.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle RemoveMeasure id` | This command removes the given measure by ID. | **Inputs:** id The measure ID. |
| [poIPost RemoveModel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_removemodel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle RemoveModel id` | This command removes the given model by ID. | **Inputs:** id The model ID. |
| [poIPost RemoveModelFromResultOverlay](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_removemodelfromresultoverlay.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `post_handle RemoveModelFromResultOverlay hwID` | This command removes the model from participating in result overlay. For these models, the plot will not be shown when contour, tensor, vector or iso is applied. | **Inputs:** hwID The ID of the model to be removed from participating in result overlay. |
| [poIPost RemoveNote](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_removenote.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle RemoveNote id` | This command removes the note with specified ID. | Not stated |
| [poIPost RemoveQueryRule](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_removequeryrule.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `client_handle RemoveQueryRule id` | This command removes query rule from the advanced query. | **Inputs:** id Accepts a rule ID. |
| [poIPost RemoveResult](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_removeresult.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `post_handle RemoveResult id context notify` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, removes a result definition with the given ID. Returns Success (0) or and error code. | **Inputs:** id The result definition ID to remove. context A string indicating the context of the result to remove (scalar, tensor, vector, iso). notify Set to 'true' to trigger an event to update the browser, otherwise 'false'. The default value is true. |
| [poIPost RemoveSection](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_removesection.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle RemoveSection id` | This command removes the given section by ID. | **Inputs:** id The section ID. |
| [poIPost SetActiveModel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_setactivemodel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle SetActiveModelHandle id` | This command sets the active model ID. | **Inputs:** id The model ID. |
| [poIPost SetBestViewTransparency](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_setbestviewtransparency.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle SetBestViewTransparency transparency` | This command sets the transparency level for all best views in the session. | **Inputs:** transparency Specify the transparency level between 0.1 and 0.9. 0.9 (highest transparency) is the default level. 0 is an invalid entry. Numeric entries below 0.1 will be treated as 0.1. Entries above will be treated as 0.9. Non-numeric entries are treated as invalid. |
| [poIPost SetBoundaryMeshMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_setboundarymeshmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle SetBoundaryMeshMode mode` | This command sets the boundary mesh mode for iso and section cuts.Note: This functionality is common for iso and section cuts. | **Inputs:** mode Set to features or none. |
| [poIPost SetDisplayOptions](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_setdisplayoptions.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle SetDisplayOptions name state` | This command sets the post display options. | **Inputs:** name The display option name: tracing, tracking, contour, isovalue, tensor, vector, legend, section cut, measure, notes, axisymmetry, and perspective. state The display option value: true for on, or false for off. |
| [poIPost SetMeasureAngleFormat](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_setmeasureangleformat.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle SetMeasureAngleFormat format` | This command sets the angle format used by all angle measures in this window. | **Inputs:** format The angle format, either degrees or radians. |
| [poIPost SetMeasureNumericFormat](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_setmeasurenumericformat.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle SetMeasureNumericFormat format` | This command sets the numeric format used by all measures in this window. | **Inputs:** format The numeric format: auto, fixed, or scientific. |
| [poIPost SetMeasureNumericPrecision](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_setmeasurenumericprecision.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle SetMeasureNumericPrecision precision` | This command sets the number of digits past the decimal to be displayed by all measures in this window. | **Inputs:** precision The numeric precision. |
| [poIPost SetMeshMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_setmeshmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle SetMeshMode mode` | This command sets the mesh mode of non-visible geometry. | **Inputs:** mode Available inputs are: meshlines/edges/features/none. |
| [poIPost SetPerspectiveAngle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_setperspectiveangle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle SetPerspectiveAngle` | This command sets the angle for the perspective view. | Not stated |
| [poIPost SetSectionDeformMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_setsectiondeformmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle SetSectionDeformMode deformable` | This command toggles the deformable section mode.Note: Deformable section cuts are planar at the simulation step when the deformable mode is activated. They stretch out of plane with the geometry in the simulations that follow. Planar sections cuts are always cut by a stationary (or undeformable) plane. | **Inputs:** deformable True Enables deformable section cuts. False Enables planar section cuts. |
| [poIPost SetSectionViewAcceleration](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_setsectionviewacceleration.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle SetSectionViewAcceleration fast` | This command toggles the deformable section mode.Note: Deformable section cuts are slow (but more accurate) by default. | **Inputs:** fast Set to true to enable accelerated section cut mode. |
| [poIPost SetTransparencyMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipost_settransparencymode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPost_handle SetTransparencyMode state` | This command sets the global flag controlling the display of non-visible geometry as transparent. | **Inputs:** state Set to true to show non-visible geometry as transparent. |
| [poIPostManager AddGraphicMaterial](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipostmanager_addgraphicmaterial.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPostManager_handle AddGraphicMaterial` | This command adds a material that describes the graphical properties of a surface, such as shininess, emmissivity, and specular color. | Not stated |
| [poIPostManager AddModel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipostmanager_addmodel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPostManager_handle AddModel filename reader_name` | This command loads the given model file. A reference to the model needs to be attached to a post client window using AddModelReference. | **Inputs:** filename The model file name. reader_name (Optional) The model reader name. |
| [poIPostManager RemoveGraphicMaterial](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipostmanager_removegraphicmaterial.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPostManager_handle RemoveGraphicMaterial` | This command removes a material from the material's list. | Not stated |
| [poIPostManager RemoveModel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipostmanager_removemodel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPostManager_handle RemoveModel modelid` | This command removes the given model from the master model table. The model is only removed if there are no other references to that model. | **Inputs:** modelid The model ID. |
| [poIPostManager ResetMouseActions](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipostmanager_resetmouseactions.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPostManager_handle ResetMouseActions` | This command restores the mouse actions to the defaults. | Not stated |
| [poIPostManager SetCachingEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipostmanager_setcachingenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPostManager_handle SetCachingEnabled state` | This command enables or disables disk caching.Note: This command takes effect only when another model is loaded. | **Inputs:** state True if the vector data can be cached or false if not. |
| [poIPostManager SetGlobalPropertyDefaults](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipostmanager_setglobalpropertydefaults.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPostManager_handle SetGlobalPropertyDefaults` | This command sets the default value to a global property. | Not stated |
| [poIPostManager SetGlobalPropertyValue](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipostmanager_setglobalpropertyvalue.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPostManager_handle SetGlobalPropertyValue property value` | This command sets the value to the global property. | **Inputs:** property The name of the available global property. value Value must be "Yes" or "No". |
| [poIPostManager SetMaximumMemoryUsage](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipostmanager_setmaximummemoryusage.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPostManager_handle SetMaximumMemoryUsage percentage` | This command sets the maximum percentage of physical memory that the post client should use. | **Inputs:** percentage A value between 20 and 100. |
| [poIPostManager SetMemoryWarningEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipostmanager_setmemorywarningenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPostManager_handle SetMemoryWarningEnabled enabled` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, enables or disables the memory usage warning feature, which will warn you when system memory reaches a certain threshold. | **Inputs:** enabled True to enable the memory usage warning feature, false to disable this feature. |
| [poIPostManager SetMemoryWarningThreshold](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipostmanager_setmemorywarningthreshold.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPostManager_handle SetMemoryWarningThreshold threshold` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, sets the current memory warning threshold value. This value is a percentage from 0-100 which represents a percentage of the total system memory. If the total system memory exceeds this threshold and the memory warning feature is enabled, a warning will trigger. | **Inputs:** threshold The memory warning threshold percentage value from 0-100. |
| [poIPostManager SetMouseAction](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipostmanager_setmouseaction.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPostManager_handle SetMouseAction button` | This command sets the given button and modifier key string to the action. | **Inputs:** button The action associated with the left, middle, and right mouse buttons. The button string can be preceded by any combination of modifier keys; ctrl, shift, ctrl-shift (for example: "ctrl left", "ctrl-shift right", etc.). |
| [poIPostManager SetPoolAliases](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipostmanager_setpoolaliases.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPostManager_handle SetPoolAliases entity_type list` | This command sets the pool aliases for pool reference resolution. | **Inputs:** entity_type Possible entity types include: node, element, part, or system. list Represents a prioritized list of aliases. |
| [poIPostManager SetReaderPropertyValue](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipostmanager_setreaderpropertyvalue.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIPostManager_handle SetReaderPropertyValue prop value reader_name` | This command allows you to set "Reader Options" by providing the option (property) name and its value, as well as the reader name. The list and the meaning of the reader options vary from reader to reader. | **Inputs:** prop The property defined by the reader. value The value to be set for the property. reader_name The name of the reader. |
| [poIPostManager WaitForResults](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poipostmanager_waitforresults.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `postmanager_handle WaitForResults type` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, causes the application to wait for all of the results of the given type to load before returning. If type is not specified, then it will wait for all of the types to finish loading before returning. It can be used as a sync point for scripting. | **Inputs:** type Optional argument specifying the type of data to wait for. Valid values are: scalar, vector, tensor, simulation, or iso. |
| [poIQueryCtrl SetDataSourceProperty](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiqueryctrl_setdatasourceproperty.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIQueryControl_handle SetDataSourceProperty source property value` | This command sets the new value for a specified property for a given data source. | **Inputs:** source The source for which the property is to be set. property The property to be set. value The value of the property to be set. |
| [poIQueryCtrl SetQuery](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiqueryctrl_setquery.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIQueryControl_handle SetQuery query` | This command sets the query string.Note: Query is a string containing a space (" ") or comma (",") separated list of datasource fields in the form "datasource.field" (example: "node.id node.coords contour.value"). | **Inputs:** query The query string to be set. Query Type Query Options Node Based node.id node.pool node.rsys node.asys node.coords component.id component.pool component.name component.color misc.load misc.sim misc.model Element Based element.id element.pool element.config element.connectivity element.normal element.centroid component.id component.pool component.name component.color misc.load misc.sim misc.model Part Based component.id component.pool component.name component.color misc.load misc.sim misc.model System Based system.id system.pool system.type system.origin system.x-axis system.y-axis system.z-axis Result Based contour.valueresult.complex_value_M/P result.complex_value_R/I result.value |
| [poIQueryCtrl SetRemoveDuplicates](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiqueryctrl_setremoveduplicates.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIQueryControl_handle SetRemoveDuplicates state` | This command sets the state of the remove duplicates option (which will remove duplicate node data queried when set to True). | **Inputs:** state Boolean value to set the remove duplicates state (True/False). |
| [poIQueryCtrl SetSelectionSet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiqueryctrl_setselectionset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIQueryControl_handle SetSelectionSet SetID` | This command sets the selection set of a query. | **Inputs:** SetID The ID of the selection set. |
| [poIQueryCtrl WriteData](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiqueryctrl_writedata.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIQueryControl_handle WriteData filename` | This command writes the query results to the specified file in .csv format. | **Inputs:** filename The complete file path. |

| [poIQueryIterator Next](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiqueryiterator_next.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIQueryIterator_handle Next` | This command moves the iterator to the next item. | Not stated |

| [poIQuerySetRule SetSelectionSet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiquerysetrule_setselectionset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `rule_handle SetSelectionSet locatorStr` | This command adds/sets the selection set to the advanced query. | **Inputs:** locatorStr Accepts the following: "pool_name id", "all", or "displayed". |

| [poIRenderOptions SetAnimateEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setanimateenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetAnimateEnabled state` | This command sets the state of Animation during Dynamic view manipulation. | **Inputs:** state Set to true to Animate during view manipulation, false otherwise. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetAntiAliasing](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setantialiasing.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetAntiAliasing aa_request` | This command sets the current anti-aliasing setting. | **Inputs:** aa_request true to enable anti-aliasing, false to disable anti-aliasing. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetAxisColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setaxiscolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetAxisColor axis color` | This command sets the color for the global axis. | **Inputs:** axis Set to x, y, or z (also X, Y, Z or 1, 2, 3). color Set the value using the RGB format (for example, 255 255 255). |
| [poIRenderOptions SetAxisLabelColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setaxislabelcolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetAxisLabelColor color` | This command sets the color of the labels for the global axis. | **Inputs:** color The color value for the labels in RGB format (for example, 255 255 255). |

| [poIRenderOptions SetCollectorColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setcollectorcolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetCollectorColor color` | This command sets the highlighting color that will be used for entity selection in HyperWorks. It corresponds to the graphical user interface option Preferences > Appearance > Selection Color.Note: It is recommended that this command only be used in HyperWorks and not in HyperWorks Desktop. | **Inputs:** color Set the color using the RGB format (for example, 255 255 255). |
| [poIRenderOptions SetCollectorTransparentMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setcollectortransparentmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetCollectorTransparentMode true` | This command sets the highlighting mode that will be in HyperWorks to transparent (true) or default (false). It corresponds to the graphical user interface option Preferences > Performance > Display > Transparent highlighting.Note: It is recommended that this command only be used in HyperWorks and not in HyperWorks Desktop. | **Inputs:** true Set the highlighting to transparent. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetContourLightingEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setcontourlightingenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetContourLightingEnabled state` | This command sets the lighting state when a contour is applied. Note: When contour lighting is disabled, the colors will not change when the display is rotated, therefore it may be easier to match the displayed colors with the legend. | **Inputs:** state Set to true to enable lighting, or false to disable it. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetCoordinateSystemSize](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setcoordinatesystemsize.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetCoordinateSystemSize size` | This command sets the size used to draw coordinate systems. | **Inputs:** size The size of the coordinate system (10 to 50 pixels). |

| [poIRenderOptions SetElementMarkShape](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setelementmarkshape.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetElementMarkShape name shape` | This command sets the element mark shape for a given element configuration. | **Inputs:** name The name of the element configuration. shape The name of the shape. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetElementMarkSize](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setelementmarksize.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetElementMarkSize size` | This command sets the element mark size in model percentage units. | **Inputs:** size The element mark size. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetEnvironmentMapping](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setenvironmentmapping.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `render_options SetEnvironmentMapping status` | This command sets the status of the environment mapping option (True, False). | Not stated |
| [poIRenderOptions SetEnvMapPathAndFormat](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setenvmappathandformat.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `render_options SetEnvMapPathAndFormat path format` | This command sets the relative path and image format of the environment textures folder. | Not stated |
| [poIRenderOptions SetErosionDrawEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_seterosiondrawenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetErosionDrawEnabled value` | This command sets the erosion draw enabled status. | **Inputs:** value True/false. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetEyeScreenRatio](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_seteyescreenratio.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetEyeScreenRatio arg1` | This command sets the ratio of distance between the viewer's eyes and the distance from the screen. | [poIRenderOptions SetFeatureAngle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setfeatureangle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetFeatureAngle degrees` | This command sets the feature or crease angle between elements. | **Inputs:** degrees Set to a value between 0 and 80. The default is 30.Note: If the angle exceeds that value, a feature line is generated. |

| [poIRenderOptions SetGeneratedLineThickness](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setgeneratedlinethickness.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetGeneratedLineThickness thickness` | This command sets the thickness of lines generated from iso value and section cut operations. | **Inputs:** thickness The thickness of the generated lines. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetGlobalAxisEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setglobalaxisenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetGlobalAxisEnabled state` | This command sets the global axis draw option. | **Inputs:** state Set to true to draw the global axis in the view, otherwise false. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetGradientType](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setgradienttype.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetGradientType type` | This command sets the gradient background layout (for example: vertical, diagonal1, etc.). | **Inputs:** type The name of the gradient type. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetLightingMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setlightingmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetLightingMode mode` | This command sets the current lighting mode. | **Inputs:** mode 0 for Single light mode. 1 for Multi-light CAE mode. 2 for Multi-light CAD mode. |

| [poIRenderOptions SetMeshColorMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setmeshcolormode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetMeshColorMode mode` | This command sets the mesh color mode (Auto, Override). | Not stated |
| [poIRenderOptions SetMeshDetails](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setmeshdetails.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetMeshDetails value` | This command sets the value of the mesh detail slider (1 to 100). A value of 100 means mesh lines are always shown, regardless of how fine the meshes are. | **Inputs:** value The mesh detail slider value (1 to 100). |
| [poIRenderOptions SetNumberOfTracingSteps](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setnumberoftracingsteps.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetNumberOfTracingSteps nsteps` | This command sets the number of simulation steps to trace. | **Inputs:** nsteps The number of simulation steps to trace. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetOptimizeRotatePanViewEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setoptimizerotatepanviewenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetOptimizeRotatePanViewEnabled status` | This command sets the status of the optimize rotate/pan option (True, False). | Not stated |
| [poIRenderOptions SetPerspectiveMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setperspectivemode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetPerspectiveMode arg1` | This command sets the current view mode: true for perspective, or false for orthographic. | **Inputs:** arg1 Set to true for perspective view, or false for orthographic view. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetPickApertureSize](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setpickaperturesize.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetPickApertureSize pixels` | This command sets the pick aperture size or tolerance for the mouse cursor. | **Inputs:** pixels Set to an integer value. The default is five. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetPreloadDataEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setpreloaddataenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetPreloadDataEnabled state` | This command sets the PreLoad Simulation Data state. | **Inputs:** state Set to true to load data first, then begin animation. Set to false to animate while loading data. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetRealisticRendering](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setrealisticrendering.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `render_options SetRealisticRendering status` | This command sets the status of the realistic rendering option (True, False). | Not stated |
| [poIRenderOptions SetReverseLegendCaptureEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setreverselegendcaptureenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetReverseLegendCaptureEnabled state` | This command sets the legend image capturing state. This option is disabled by default. | **Inputs:** state If true, the legend background is reversed during image capture. If false, the legend background is left as is during image capture. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetReverseVideoCaptureEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setreversevideocaptureenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetReverseVideoCaptureEnabled state` | This command sets the image capturing state. | **Inputs:** state Set to true to enable reverse video capturing, otherwise false. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetScaleCaptureAnnotations](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setscalecaptureannotations.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetScaleCaptureAnnotations state` | This command sets whether to scale annotations during image capture. | **Inputs:** state Set to 'true' to scale annotations during image capture, otherwise 'false'. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetShadeMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setshademode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetShadeMode mode` | This command sets the shading mode. | **Inputs:** mode Set to flat or smooth. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetShowAxisArrows](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setshowaxisarrows.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetShowAxisArrows show` | This command sets the display state of the triad axis arrows. | **Inputs:** show True to show the triad axis arrows, false to not show the triad axis arrows. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetSpecularColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setspecularcolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetSpecularColor color` | This command sets the specular color. | **Inputs:** color Set to a value in the form of "abc abc abc" (where abc is a value between 0 and 255). |
| [poIRenderOptions SetSpringAsCyclinderCoilCount](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setspringascyclindercoilcount.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetSpringAsCyclinderCoilCount num_coils` | This command sets the number of spring coils beyond which a cylinder will be generated (rather than a spring). | **Inputs:** num_coils The number of spring coils beyond which a cylinder will be generated (rather than a spring). This function affects newly generated geometry and will have no effect on currently loaded springs. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetStereo](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setstereo.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetStereo arg1` | This command enables the stereoscopic (3D) display if arg1 is set to true and the environment variable HW_STEREO is set to 1. If arg1 is set to false, the stereoscopic (3D) display is disabled. | **Inputs:** arg1 Set to true to enable the stereoscopic display (provided the environment variable HW_STEREO is also set to 1), or set to false to disable the stereoscopic display. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetStereoPerspectiveAngle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setstereoperspectiveangle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetStereoPerspectiveAngle arg1` | This command sets the perspective field of view angle when the stereoscopic display is enabled. | **Inputs:** arg1 The field of view angle in degrees. The valid range is from 5 to 45. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetUndeformedColorMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setundeformedcolormode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetUndeformedColorMode mode` | This command sets the undeformed shape color mode. | **Inputs:** mode Set to component, mesh, or user. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetUndeformedMeshMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setundeformedmeshmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetUndeformedMeshMode mode` | This command sets the undeformed shape mesh mode. | **Inputs:** mode Set to none, edges, featues, or wireframe. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetUndeformedTrackEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setundeformedtrackenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetUndeformedTrackEnabled state` | This command sets the undeformed track enabled state.Note: This option is valid only if a tracking system had been defined. | **Inputs:** state Set to true to make the undeformed shape move with the tracking system. Set to false to allow the undeformed shape to move freely. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetUndeformedUserColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setundeformedusercolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetUndeformedUserColor color` | This command sets the color for undeformed shape. Note: This option is valid only if a tracking system had been defined. | **Inputs:** color Set the color value using the RGB format (for example, 255 255 255). |
| [poIRenderOptions SetUseModelCenter](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setusemodelcenter.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetUseModelCenter state` | This command sets whether to use the model center for keyboard rotations. | **Inputs:** state Set to 'true' to use the model center for keyboard rotations or 'false' to use the screen center. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetViewAccelerationEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setviewaccelerationenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetViewAccelerationEnabled state` | This command sets the view acceleration state. | **Inputs:** state Set to true to enable view acceleration, otherwise false. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetViewCubeDisplay](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setviewcubedisplay.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetViewCubeDisplay display_mode` | This command sets the view cube display state. | **Inputs:** display_mode Set to "never", "mouseover", or "always". |
| [poIRenderOptions SetViewCubeGeometry](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setviewcubegeometry.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetViewCubeGeometry geometry` | This command sets the view cube geometry state. | **Inputs:** geometry Set to "chamfered cube" or "simple cube". Alternatively, “chamfered” or “simple” may also be used. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetViewCubeOrientation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setviewcubeorientation.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetViewCubeOrientation orientation` | This command sets the view cube front face orientation. | **Inputs:** orientation Set to “XY”, “-XY”, “XZ”, “-XZ”, “YX”, “-YX”, “YZ”, “-YZ”, “ZX”, “-ZX”, “ZY” or “-ZY”. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetViewCubePosition](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setviewcubeposition.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetViewCubePosition position` | This command sets the view cube position. | **Inputs:** position Set to "lower left", "lower right", "upper left" or "upper right". Alternatively, "lowleft", "lowright", "upleft" or "upright" may also be used. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetViewCubeReflectors](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setviewcubereflectors.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetViewCubeReflectors reflectors` | This command sets the view cube reflectors display state. | **Inputs:** reflectors Set to "never", "mouseover", or "always". |
| [poIRenderOptions SetViewCubeRotatorIncrement](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setviewcuberotatorincrement.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetViewCubeRotatorIncrement increment` | This command sets the view cube rotator increment angle. | **Inputs:** reflectors Rotator angle in degrees. |
| [poIRenderOptions SetViewCubeRotators](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setviewcuberotators.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetViewCubeRotators rotators` | This command sets the view cube rotators display state. | **Inputs:** rotators Set to "never", "mouseover", or "always". |
| [poIRenderOptions SetZoomFactor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderoptions_setzoomfactor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIRenderOptions_handle SetZoomFactor factor` | This command sets the zoom factor. | **Inputs:** factor The zoom factor. |
| [poIResultCtrl AddSubcase](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_addsubcase.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle AddSubcase label` | This command adds a derived subcase to a result.Note: A derived subcase can reference simulations from other subcases. | **Inputs:** label The label associated with the derived subcase. |
| [poIResultCtrl AppendSimulation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poirenderctrl_appendsimulation.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle AppendSimulation subcase_id simulation_index` | This command adds a simulation reference to the current subcase.Note: AppendSimulation will fail if the current subcase is not a derived subcase.A simulation can be added to a derived subcase only once. | **Inputs:** subcase_id The ID of the subcase which contains the source simulation. simulation_index The source simulation index. |
| [poIResultCtrl CancelLoading](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_cancelloading.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle CancelLoading notify` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, cancels all pending load tasks associated with this result control. Returns Success (0) if cancelling was successful or Warning (2) if there was a problem during cancelling. | **Inputs:** notify Set to 'true' to trigger an event to update the browser, otherwise 'false'. The default value is true. |
| [poIResultCtrl CancelResult](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_cancelresult.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle CancelResult id context notify` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, cancels all future tasks, clears and unloads the specified result definition. Returns Success (0) if cancelling was successful or Warning (2) if there was a problem cancelling. | **Inputs:** id The ID of the result definition to cancel. context A string indicating the context of the request. Valid values are: scalar, tensor, vector, and iso. notify Set to 'true' to trigger an event to update the browser, otherwise 'false'. The default value is true. |
| [poIResultCtrl ClearPlot](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_clearplot.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle ClearPlot id context notify` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, clears the plot of the specified result definition. Returns Success (0) if clearing was successful or Warning (2) if there was a problem during clearing. | **Inputs:** id The ID of the result definition to clear. context A string indicating the context of the request. Valid values are: scalar, tensor, vector, and iso. notify Set to 'true' to trigger an event to update the browser, otherwise 'false'. The default value is true. |
| [poIResultCtrl CreateLayerFilter](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_createlayerfilter.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle CreateLayerFilter subcase_id data_type_name layer_filter_id` | This command creates a layer filter for a given subcase and data type (with a given filter ID). If a filter ID is not provided, the filter will be created starting from 1. A filter will not be created if the given data type does not have more than one layer. Also, a filter will not be created if another filter already exist with same ID. The layer filter will automatically be created and is given the default name LayerFilter "n” (with "n" being the next number in the series). By default all the layers of the data type will be stored in the layer filter. | **Inputs:** subcase_id The ID of the subcase for which the layer filter has to be created. data_type_name The data type name from which the filter has to be created. layer_filter_id The ID of the layer filter to be created. |
| [poIResultCtrl EraseSubcase](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_erasesubcase.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle EraseSubcase subcase_id` | This command erases a derived subcase from a result. Note: Only derived subcases (those created with AddSubcase) can be erased. | **Inputs:** subcase_id The ID of a derived subcase. |
| [poIResultCtrl LoadResult](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_loadresult.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle LoadResult id context` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, loads data for the specified result definition. Returns Success (0) if loading was successful or Warning (2) if there was a problem loading the result data. | **Inputs:** id The ID of the result definition to load. context A string indicating the context of the request. Valid values are: scalar, tensor, vector, and iso. |
| [poIResultCtrl LoadSimulation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_loadsimulation.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle LoadSimulation subcase_id index` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, loads position data (animation) for the given subcase and frame (index). | **Inputs:** subcase_id The ID of the subcase or 0 for all subcases. index The frame number (0 based) or -1 for all frames. |
| [poIResultCtrl PlotResult](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_plotresult.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle PlotResult id context notify` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, plots the specified result definition. Returns Success (0) if plotting is successful or Warning (2) if there was a problem during plotting. | **Inputs:** id The ID of the result definition to plot. context A string indicating the context of the request. Valid values are: scalar, tensor, vector, and iso. notify Set to 'true' to trigger an event to update the browser, otherwise 'false'. The default value is true. |
| [poIResultCtrl RemoveLayerFilter](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_removelayerfilter.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle RemoveLayerFilter layer_filter_id` | This command removes the layer filter with the given ID from the layer filters available in the result control. | **Inputs:** layer_filter_id The ID of the layer filter to be removed. |
| [poIResultCtrl RemoveSimulation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_removesimulation.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle RemoveSimulation simulation_index` | This command removes the simulation reference from the current subcase.Note: RemoveSimulation will fail if the current subcase is not a derived subcase. | **Inputs:** simulation_index The simulation index to remove. |
| [poIResultCtrl SetCurrentSimulation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_setcurrentsimulation.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle SetCurrentSimulation index` | This command sets the current transient simulation. | **Inputs:** index Set to an integer greater than or equal to zero. |
| [poIResultCtrl SetCurrentSubcase](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_setcurrentsubcase.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle SetCurrentSubcase id` | This command sets the current subcase for result manipulation operations (for example: animation, contour, and vector plot). | **Inputs:** id Set to a positive integer. |
| [poIResultCtrl SetDisplaySelectionSet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_setdisplayselectionset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle SetDisplaySelectionSet context idstr` | This command sets a selection set to the result controller for a given context which will be used to display the plots on those entities in the set. | **Inputs:** context The context for which display selection set is to be applied. Valid options include: scalar, vector, tensor, and iso. idstr The string which fully defines the selection set. It is a locator string which may be a pool ID string. |
| [poIResultCtrl SetSubcaseLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_setsubcaselabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl SetSubcaseLabel subcase_id label` | This command sets the label of a derived subcase. | **Inputs:** subcase_id The ID of the derived subcase. label The new label for the derived subcase. |
| [poIResultCtrl UnloadResult](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_unloadresult.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle UnloadResult id context notify` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, clears and unloads data for the specified result definition. Returns Success (0) if unloading was successful or Warning (2) if there was a problem unloading the result data. | **Inputs:** id The ID of the result definition to unload. context A string indicating the context of the request. Valid values are: scalar, tensor, vector, and iso. notify Set to 'true' to trigger an event to update the browser, otherwise 'false'. The default value is true. |
| [poIResultCtrl UnloadSimulation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_unloadsimulation.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle UnloadSimulation index` | This command frees memory associated with the given simulation index.Note: An index of -1 unloads all steps but the current step. | **Inputs:** index The simulation index. |
| [poIResultCtrl UpdateResult](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultctrl_updateresult.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIResultCtrl_handle UpdateResult id context` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, forces an update to the specified result definition by loading the result data for any newly loaded steps. This can be used in a situation where you load the results for a subset of simulation steps and then proceed to load additional simulation steps afterwards. Calling this API will cause the results to be loaded for the newly loaded simulation steps. Returns Success (0) if updating was successful or Warning (2) if there was a problem during updating. | **Inputs:** id The ID of the result definition to update. context A string indicating the context of the request. Valid values are: scalar, tensor, vector, and iso. |
| [poIResultMathCtrl AddExpression](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultmathctrl_addexpression.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `rmath_handle AddExpression label` | This command adds a new empty result math expression. If the result math expression is properly set up, a user-defined data type will be created. | **Inputs:** label (Optional) Label for the expression. |
| [poIResultMathCtrl AddResource](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultmathctrl_addresource.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `rmath_handle AddResource res_name file_path` | This command adds an external resource file to the current model. An external resource file is typically an xml file which contains additional tables like: material yield, thickness table, etc. This command is generally used when you would like to access some of these external table values along with tables from the model/result file. | **Inputs:** res_name The name for the resource (which must be unique). file_path The complete path of the file name. |
| [poIResultMathCtrl Commit](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultmathctrl_commit.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `rmath_handle Commit` | This command creates the user-defined data type. | Not stated |
| [poIResultMathCtrl RemoveExpression](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultmathctrl_removeexpression.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `rmath_handle RemoveExpression id` | This command deletes/removes an existing result math expression. If there is a data type corresponding to this expression, the data type will also be deleted. | **Inputs:** id The ID of the expression to be deleted/removed. |
| [poIResultMathCtrl SetEquation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultmathctrl_setequation.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `rmathexp_handle SetEquation eqn mode` | This command sets the math equation. | **Inputs:** eqn A valid equation string. mode (Optional) The mode can have a value of "alias" or "original". The default value is "alias". |
| [poIResultMathCtrl SetLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultmathctrl_setlabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `rmathexp_handle SetLabel label` | This command sets the label of the result math expression. | **Inputs:** label The label for the expression. The label cannot be the name of an existing data type. In addition, the label cannot contain any of the following characters: '[',']','{','}','\'','\','<','>','&', ':'. |
| [poIResultMathCtrl SetSubcase](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiresultmathctrl_setsubcase.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `rmathexp_handle SetSubcase subid` | This command sets the subcase ID in which the data type will be created. | **Inputs:** subid The ID of the subcase. A value of zero means that the data type will be created in all of the subcases. |
| [poIScalarDefine SetAverageFactor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiscalardefine_setaveragefactor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIScalarDefine_handle SetAverageFactor f` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, sets the nodal variation factor, which specifies the level above which nodal averaging should not be carried out.Note: The result definition can only be modified if it is not cached. | **Inputs:** f The threshold level between 0.0 and 100.0. |
| [poIScalarDefine SetAverageFactorEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiscalardefine_setaveragefactorenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIScalarDefine_handle SetAverageFactorEnabled enable` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, sets the nodal variation factor enable state for this result definition.Note: The result definition can only be modified if it is not cached. | **Inputs:** enable True to enable nodal variation, false to disable. |
| [poIScalarDefine SetAverageMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiscalardefine_setaveragemode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIScalarDefine_handle SetAverageMode mode` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, sets the averaging mode for the result definition.Note: The result definition can only be modified if it is not cached. | **Inputs:** mode The averaging mode. The mode must be one of the following: “none”, “simple”, “advanced”, “difference”, “maximum”, “minimum”, “max of corner”, “min or corner” or “extreme of corner”. |
| [poIScalarDefine SetAvgAcrossPartsEnable](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiscalardefine_setavgacrosspartsenable.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIScalarDefine_handle SetAvgAcrossPartsEnable enable` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, sets the average across parts enable state.Note: The result definition can only be modified if it not cached. | **Inputs:** enable True to enable averaging across parts, false to disable. |
| [poIScalarDefine SetComplexFilter](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiscalardefine_setcomplexfilter.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIScalarDefine_handle SetComplexFilter filter` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, sets the complex filter for the result definition.Note: The result definition can only be modified if it is not cached. | **Inputs:** filter The new complex filter. It must be one of the following: “mag * cos(wt-phase)”, “mag * cos(wt+phase)”, “mag”, “phase”, “real” or “imaginary”. |

Returns Success (0) or an error code.Note: The result definition can only be modified if it is not cached. | **Inputs:** list The list of multiple layers of the form: "<top>:<bottom>", " <top>;<bottom> " or "<top>\\|<bottom>". |

The base time for the deformable section will go to the closest frame in the current animator for the page. | **Inputs:** time The time value in seconds. |
| [poISection SetClipAbove](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setclipabove.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetClipAbove state` | This command toggles the current direction of the clipping surface for the section. Set to "True" to clip above the section surface, or "False" to clip below. | **Inputs:** state The state for the current clipping direction. |
