| [poISection SetColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setcolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetColor color` | This command sets the section cut color. | **Inputs:** color The section cut color. If the section color override in enabled, the section geometry is drawn in this color. |
| [poISection SetColorOverride](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setcoloroverride.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetColorOverride override` | This command sets the state of the color override option for a section. | **Inputs:** override Set to true to change (override) the section color, otherwise false. |
| [poISection SetCrossSectionOnly](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setcrosssectiononly.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetCrossSectionOnly state` | This command sets the cross section only state. | **Inputs:** state If the cross section is visible, the state is true. If the cross section is not visible, the state is false. |
| [poISection SetD](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setd.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetD` | This command sets the plane "D" parameter for a Planar section type. | Not stated |
| [poISection SetDeformMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setdeformmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetDeformable deformable` | This command toggles the deformable section mode.Note: Deformable section cuts are planar at the simulation step when the deformable mode is activated and stretch out of the plane with the geometry in simulations which follow or precede. Planar section cuts are always cut by a stationary (or undeformable) plane. | **Inputs:** deformable Set to true to enable the deformable section cut mode. Set to false for planar section cuts. |
| [poISection SetDIncrement](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setdincrement.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetDIncrement increment` | This command sets the amount to increment the plane's "D" when translating the section along the normal for a Planar section type. | **Inputs:** state The value of the increment. |
| [poISection SetDisplayOption](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setdisplayoption.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetDisplayOption name value` | This command returns the state of the given section display option. | **Inputs:** name The name of the section display option: “gridlines” Display of grid lines. “grixtext” Display of values around the grid. value The boolean value for the section display option. |
| [poISection SetGridSpacingX](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setgridspacingx.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetGridSpacingX distance` | This command sets the distance on the local X axis in generic model units for a Planar section type. | **Inputs:** distance The distance between two grid lines. |
| [poISection SetGridSpacingY](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setgridspacingy.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetGridSpacingY distance` | This command sets the distance on the local Y axis in generic model units for a Planar section type. | **Inputs:** distance The distance between two grid lines. |
| [poISection SetGridTextPrecision](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setgridtextprecision.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetGridTextPrecision precision` | This command sets the precision of each distance value displayed along the local X and Y axes of the cross section's grid for a Planar section type. | **Inputs:** precision The number of significant digits (digits following the decimal point). |
| [poISection SetLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setlabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetLabel label` | This command sets the section label. | **Inputs:** label The section label. |
| [poISection SetOrientation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setorientation.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetOrientation plane_normal` | This command sets the section's orientation plane for a Planar section type. | **Inputs:** plane_normal The orientation plane normal as a string. |
| [poISection SetOrientationMethod](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setorientationmethod.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetOrientationMethod mode` | This command sets the section orientation method for a Planar section type. | **Inputs:** mode The section orientation method. Options include: n1n2n3base, xaxis, yaxis, zaxis, normaltoscreen, or user. |
| [poISection SetOrientationNode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setorientationnode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetOrientationNode plane_normal` | This command sets the section's orientation plane by model/node ID pairs for a Planar section type. This command accepts two formats: Two modelID/nodeID pairs define a vector: "1 204 1 205" defines a vector between nodes 204 and 205 on model 1. Three modelID/nodeID pairs define a plane: "1 204 1 1008 2 101" defines a plane through nodes 204 and 208 on model 1, and node 101 on model 2. | **Inputs:** plane_normal The orientation plane normal as a string. |
| [poISection SetRadius](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisphericalsection_setradius.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetRadius value` | This command sets the clipping sphere radius for the section. | **Inputs:** value The radius to be applied to the clipping sphere. |
| [poISection SetThickness](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setthickness.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetThickness thickness` | This command sets the thickness of the section cut edge lines. Only valid when running HyperWorks. | **Inputs:** thickness Thickness value from 1-6 or “global” to use the global section cut thickness as defined in the preferences. |
| [poISection SetVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setvisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetVisibility state` | This command sets the section visibility. | **Inputs:** state True If the section is visible. False If the section is not visible. |
| [poISection SetWidth](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisection_setwidth.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISection_handle SetWidth` | This command sets the width of a cross section for a Planar section type. | Not stated |
| [poISelectionSet Add](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiselectionset_add.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `selectionSet_handle Add <PoolName> selection Note: PoolName only applies to: id, idlist, selectionset, MaxofSet, and MinofSet.If the <PoolName> is not specified, then the User_Set pool is considered the default.` | This command adds entities specified by the argument selection to the current selection set (for example, Add "id 1", Add "id = 1"). | **Inputs:** selection To see a detailed list of possibilities use the GetSelectByList command on the selection set (and also take a look at the list and examples below): "id" Selects entities based on a single id or range of ids. IDs can also be selected using operators like: <, <=, >, >=. "idlist" Comma separated list of IDs and ID ranges. IDs can also be selected using operators like: <, <=, >, >=. "all" Selects all of the entities and adds them to the set. The entities that are added depend upon the SetSelectMode flag. "displayed" Selects the displayed entities and adds them to the set. "component" Selects entities belonging to a component id. "component_name" Selects entities belonging to a component name. "contour" Selects entities based on a contour value. The contour value can also be combined with operators like <, <=, >, >=, as well as keywords like "Top N", "Bottom N", "MaxofSet", and "MinofSet" (see the contour section of the example below). "selectionset" Selects entities base on another selection set. "sphere" Selects entities which lie inside the specified sphere center and radius. "adjacent" Selects entities which are adjacent to the existing entities in the set. One layer of adjacency is used. "attached" Selects entities which are attached to the existing entities in the set. This selects all of the entities recursively until no attached entities are found. "dimension" Selects entities based on a specified dimension (for example, 1d elements, 2d elements, or 3d elements). "config" Selects elements based on the element configuration. For example, "config 103" means tria elements. This configuration matches with the HyperMesh configuration. The following configurations are available in HyperView: 1D 1-MASS; 2-PLOT; 3-WELD; 5-RIGID; 21-SPRING; 22-JOINT; 55-RIGIDLINK; 56-RBE3; 60-BAR; 61-ROD; 63-BAR3; 70-GAP 2D 103-TRIA3; 104-QUAD4; 106-TRIA6; 108-QUAD8 3D 204-TETRA4; 205-PYRAMID5; 206-PENTA6; 208-HEX8; 210-TETRA10; 213- PYRAMID13; 215-PENTA15; 220-HEX20; "face" Selects entities on the face of the entities that are already selected in the current selection set. This rule assumes that there is at least one entity already existing in the set. "plane" Selects entities on a plane with a given normal direction, base point coordinates and a tolerance value. In addition to these rules, a Reverse rule can be used for appropriate entities which will reverse the current selection in the set. Please follow its usage in the example. |
| [poISelectionSet Reverse](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiselectionset_reverse.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `selectionSet_handle Reverse` | This command reverses the contents of the current selection set. It affects only entities of the same type. | Not stated |
| [poISelectionSet SetColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiselectionset_setcolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `selectionSet_handle SetColor color` | This command sets the color for the visibility of the selection set. It affects the displaying/highlighting of a visible selection set and their possibly visible entity IDs. | **Inputs:** color Either the RGB value of the color or the name of it (for example, SetColor “red”; SetColor “255 0 0”). |
| [poISelectionSet SetDrawSize](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiselectionset_setdrawsize.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `selectionSet_handle SetDrawSize drawSize` | This command sets a particular draw size for the current selection set. | **Inputs:** drawSize Basically, the draw size is a value in pixels. If you use zero (0) as an argument, the default value will be set. |
| [poISelectionSet SetDrawStyle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiselectionset_setdrawstyle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `selectionSet_handle SetDrawStyle drawStyle` | This command sets a particular draw style on the current selection set. | **Inputs:** drawStyle A valid draw style is dependent on the selection set 'type'. To obtain a list of valid draw styles for the selection set, use the GetDrawStyleList command on the current selection set. |
| [poISelectionSet SetIDVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiselectionset_setidvisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `selectionSet_handle SetIDVisibility visibility_state` | This command sets the visibility of IDs for all entities included in the current selection set. This setting determines whether the IDs should be shown or not shown when graphics are updated. If the visibility state is true, the IDs for all entities are displayed. If the visibility state is false, the IDs are not displayed.Note: SetIDVisibility works only if SetVisibility was set to true before. | **Inputs:** visibility_state Set to true to display the IDs, otherwise set to false. |
| [poISelectionSet SetLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiselectionset_setlabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `selectionSet_handle SetLabel label` | This command sets the label of the current selection set. | **Inputs:** label Determines the label on the current selection set. |
| [poISelectionSet SetSelectMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiselectionset_setselectmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `selectionSet_handle SetSelectionMode mode` | This command sets the selection mode for the selection set. | **Inputs:** mode One of the possibilities listed by using GetSelectionModeList on the selection set. It can either be true if the selection set should be displayed highlighted, or false if it should not be highlighted. |
| [poISelectionSet SetVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poiselectionset_setvisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `selectionSet_handle SetVisibility visibility_state` | This command sets the visibility of the current selection set entities. This setting determines whether the current selection set should be displayed highlighted or not highlighted when graphics are updated. If the visibility state is true, the selection set entities are emphasized, If the visibility state is false, the selected entities are not emphasized on the current model. | **Inputs:** visibility_state Set to true to highlight the current selection set, set to false to not highlight it. |
| [poIStressLinearizationCtrl ComputeLinearizedStressComponents](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poistresslinearizationctrl_computelinearizedstresscomponents.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIStressLinearizationCtrl_handle ComputeLinearizedStressComponents id` | This command generates the linearized stress components for the SCL specified by ID. | **Inputs:** id The ID of the Stress Characterization Line (SCL). |
| [poIStressLinearizationCtrl RemoveSCL](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poistresslinearizationctrl_removescl.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIStressLinearizationCtrl_handle RemoveSCL id` | This command deletes the Stress Characterization Line (SCL) specified by ID. | **Inputs:** id The ID of the Stress Characterization Line (SCL). |
| [poIStressLinearizationCtrl SetComponentsToPlot](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poistresslinearizationctrl_setcomponentstoplot.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIStressLinearizationCtrl_handle SetComponentsToPlot compsToPlot` | This command specifies the tensor components to be included in the HyperGraph plot. | [poIStressLinearizationCtrl SetCreateReport](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poistresslinearizationctrl_setcreatereport.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIStressLinearizationCtrl_handle SetCreateReport flag` | This command enables report creation. The default value is false. | **Inputs:** flag Enables/disables the report creation (true/false). |
| [poIStressLinearizationCtrl SetDatatype](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poistresslinearizationctrl_setdatatype.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIStressLinearizationCtrl_handle SetDatatype type` | This command sets the data type to be used for Stress Linearization. The first stress data type found will be listed as the default. | **Inputs:** type The data type to be used. |
| [poIStressLinearizationCtrl SetInvariantsToPlot](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poistresslinearizationctrl_setinvariantstoplot.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIStressLinearizationCtrl_handle SetInvariantsToPlot invariantsToPlot` | This command specifies the invariants to be included in the HyperGraph plot. | **Inputs:** invariantsToPlot The invariants to be included in the plot. |
| [poIStressLinearizationCtrl SetPlotLayout](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poistresslinearizationctrl_setplotlayout.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIStressLinearizationCtrl_handle SetPlotLayout plotLayout` | This command sets the plot layout. The default value is One plot per line. | **Inputs:** plotLayout The plot layout type. |
| [poIStressLinearizationCtrl SetPlotOption](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poistresslinearizationctrl_setplotoption.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIStressLinearizationCtrl_handle SetPlotOption option` | This command sets the plot option to be used for Stress Linearization and Strain Linearization. "Extracted and Linearized Values" is the default plot option. This plot option is not applicable for the "Extract Data" run method. | **Inputs:** option Plot options available are: "Extracted and Linearized Values" and "Linearized Values". |
| [poIStressLinearizationCtrl SetPlotWindow](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poistresslinearizationctrl_setplotwindow.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIStressLinearizationCtrl_handle SetPlotWindow windowID` | This command sets the plot window ID. This window will be used to plot curves. | **Inputs:** windowID The window ID. |
| [poIStressLinearizationCtrl SetReportFile](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poistresslinearizationctrl_setreportfile.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIStressLinearizationCtrl_handle SetReportFile report_file` | This command sets the path for the CSV report. The default value is <model_path>/<model_name>.csv. | **Inputs:** report_file The name of the report file. |
| [poIStressLinearizationCtrl SetRunOption](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poistresslinearizationctrl_setrunoption.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIStressLinearizationCtrl_handle SetRunOption method` | This command sets the run method to be used. "Stress Linearization" is the default method. | **Inputs:** method Methods available are: "Extract Data", "Stress Linearization", and "Strain Linearization". |
| [poIStressLinearizationCtrl SetUseCurrentContour](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poistresslinearizationctrl_setusecurrentcontour.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIStressLinearizationCtrl_handle SetUseCurrentContour useCurrent` | This command instructs the Stress linearization controller whether or not to use the currently contoured datatype for stress linearization. | **Inputs:** useCurrent The currently contoured datatype to be used. |

| [poISubcase SetLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisubcase_setlabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `subcase_handle SetLabel label` | This command sets a label for the derived subcase. | [poISymmetry SetMirrorSystem](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisymmetry_setmirrorsystem.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `sym_handle SetMirrorSystem sys_str` | This command assigns the system which will be used for the mirror operation. | **Inputs:** sys_str The locator string for the system. | [poISymmetry SetOrder](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisymmetry_setorder.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `sym_handle SetOrder code` | This command sets the sequence of the various symmetry operation like reflect, rectangular copy, and circular copy. | **Inputs:** code The integer value which controls the sequence of the various symmetry operations. See the list below for the possible values and meaning of each code: 0 mirror, rect pattern, circ pattern 1 mirror, circ pattern, rect pattern 2 rect pattern, mirror, circ pattern 3 rect pattern, circ pattern, mirror 4 circ pattern, rect pattern, mirror 5 circ pattern, mirror, rect pattern |
| [poISymmetry SetPatternSystem](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisymmetry_setpatternsystem.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `sym_handle SetPatternSystem sys_str` | This command assigns the system which will be used for the rectangular and circular copy operation. | **Inputs:** sys_str The locator string for the system. If the Global system is needed, enter a value of “0”. |
| [poISymmetry SetRectPattern](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisymmetry_setrectpattern.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `sym_handle SetRectPattern axis param` | This command assigns the parameters which were used for a rectangular copy. | **Inputs:** axis The axis for which the parameters are requested. Possible values for this argument are 0/1/2 (corresponding to x/y/z axis). param The parameter string which defines the rectangular copy. This argument typically contains two values: 1) the number of entities and 2) the spacing between the entities. |
| [poISymmetry SetSelectionSet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisymmetry_setselectionset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `sym_handle SetSelectionSet idstr` | This command sets a selection set to the symmetry controller. | **Inputs:** idstr The string which fully defines the selection set. It is a locator string which may also be a pool ID string. |

| [poISystem SetIDVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisystem_setidvisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISystem_handle SetIDVisibility state` | This command sets the coordinate system's ID visibility. | **Inputs:** state True if the ID is visible, false if it is not. |
| [poISystem SetOrientation](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisystem_setorientation.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `system_handle SetOrientation orient_st` | This command sets the orientation of the coordinate system. The orientation can be set for user-defined systems only! The orientation string must contain nine values (three direction cosines are needed for defining each axes of the system). | **Inputs:** orient_st The orientation string representing the direction cosines of the axes of the system. |
| [poISystem SetOrientationByCoords](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisystem_setorientationbycoords.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `system_handle SetOrientationByCoords coord_string` | This command sets the orientation of the coordinate system using the coordinates of three nodes. The orientation can be set for user-defined systems only! The first three values define node1, the next three values define the location of node2, and the final three values define the location of node3. | **Inputs:** coord_string The string representing the coordinates of the three nodes defining the system. |
| [poISystem SetOrientationByNode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisystem_setorientationbynode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `system_handle SetOrientationByNode node1 node2 node3 [movetype]` | This command sets the orientation of the coordinate system using the coordinates of three nodes. The orientation can be set for user-defined systems only! Node1 defines the origin of the system, the vector between node1 and node2 defines the X-axis of the system, and node3 defines a point on the XY plane. | **Inputs:** node1 The ID of node1. node2 The ID of node2. node3 The ID of node3. [movetype] The move type of the system: true - Fixed (Default), false - Moving. |
| [poISystem SetOrientationByNodeMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisystem_setorientationbynodemode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `system_handle SetOrientationByNodeMode mode` | This command sets the orientation mode for the coordinate system using three nodes. The orientation mode can be set only for the user defined systems. Mode can be set only for the rectangular system. The mode can be “x-xy”, “x-xz”, “y-yx”, “y-yz”, “z-zx”, “z-zy”. If the mode is not set, the default mode “x-xy” is assumed. In addition, the mode must set before setting the orientation. | **Inputs:** mode The mode to be set. |
| [poISystem SetOrigin](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisystem_setorigin.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `system_handle SetOrigin origin_str` | This command sets the origin of the coordinate system. The origin can be set for user-defined systems only! | **Inputs:** origin_str The X, Y, and Z coordinates of the system origin. |
| [poISystem SetUserDefined](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisystem_setuserdefined.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `system_handle SetUserDefined state` | This command sets the system to be of the type "user-defined". This command is used for internal purposes only! It is highly recommended that it not be used in custom scripts, as it may become obsolete in future versions. | **Inputs:** state This argument can have a value of "true" or "false". |
| [poISystem SetVisibility](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisystem_setvisibility.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poISystem_handle SetVisibility state` | This command sets the coordinate system's visibility. | **Inputs:** state True if visible, false if it is not. |
| [poISystemCtrl SetEnableState](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisystemctrl_setenablestate.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `sysctrl_handle SetEnableState enable_state` | This command sets the enable state of the system review control. If the enable state is set to "true", then the system review plot can be seen. If the enable state is set to "false", then the system review plot is not visible and the model is drawn as it was. However, the visibility of the system review plot also depends on the display option state in the client handle; in order for the system review plot to be visible, both states must to be set to "true" (client_handle SetDisplayOptions system). | **Inputs:** enable_state Set to "true" to enable the system review. |
| [poISystemCtrl SetEntitySystemType](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisystemctrl_setentitysystemtype.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `sysctrl_handle SetEntitySystemType system_type_name` | This command sets the system type to be reviewed in a system review plot (element, material, or ply). The data type must be element system, material system, or ply system. | **Inputs:** system_type_name The name of the system type to be set. |
| [poISystemCtrl SetLayer](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisystemctrl_setlayer.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `sysctrl_handle SetLayer layer_name` | This command sets the layer to be applied for a ply system review plot (for example - Ply 1, Ply 10001001, etc.). In the case of an element and material system review plot, this command will be ignored. If the layer name contains spaces. the name must be specified inside double quotes (for example - “Ply 1”). The layer name must exist in the list of available layers. You can get this list from the result control by using the GetLayerList command. | **Inputs:** layer_name The name of the layer to be set. |
| [poISystemCtrl SetSystemLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poisystemctrl_setsystemlabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `sysctrl_handle SetSystemLabel label` | This command sets the label of the system control handle. Typically this command is used to set a label for the system (for example: material, ply, elemental, etc.). | **Inputs:** label One character only. |
| [poITensorCtrl RenamePlotStyle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poitensorctrl_renameplotstyle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poITensorCtrl_handle RenamePlotStyle old_style_name new_style_name` | This command renames an existing/old plot style with a new name.Note: The new plot style name must be unique. | **Inputs:** old_style_name The name of the old/existing plot style. new_style_name The new name to be given to the old/existing plot style. |
| [poITensorCtrl RestorePlotStyle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poitensorctrl_restoreplotstyle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poITensorCtrl_handle RestorePlotStyle style_name` | This command updates the tensor controller settings with the new settings specified by the plot style name. | **Inputs:** style_name The name of the plot style. |

| [poITensorCtrl SetColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poitensorctrl_setcolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poITensorCtrl_handle SetColor name colorvals` | This command sets the color for a given tensor component. | **Inputs:** name The tensor component name. | [poITensorCtrl SetColorMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poitensorctrl_setcolormode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poITensorCtrl_handle SetColorMode name` | This command sets the color mode for tensors.Note: Set to "legend" if tensors should use the legend. Otherwise, set to "rgb". | Not stated |
| [poITensorCtrl SetCornerDataEnabled](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poitensorctrl_setcornerdataenabled.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poITensorCtrl_handle SetCornerDataEnabled cornerdata` | This command sets the flag to indicate if corner data is requested. | **Inputs:** cornerdata True Corner data is requested. False Corner data is not requested. |

This only effective when a single curve is set to true and 'Curve by Component' is set to true. | **Inputs:** state true: curves of a component for each frame are all drawn in the component color in a graph. false: curves' color will be assigned by default in a graph. |

| [poITracingCtrl SortNodes](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poitracingctrl_sortnodes.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poITracingCtrl_handle SortNodes rule` | This command rearranges all tracing nodes based on user provided rules. | **Inputs:** rule "x”/ "y” / "z”: sort nodes based on its x,y,z coordinates in ascending order. "x+”/ "y+” / "z+”: sort nodes based on its x,y,z coordinates in ascending order. "x-”/ "y-” / "z-”: sort nodes based on its x,y,z coordinates in descending order. "sp”: sort nodes by the shortest path for each component, starting node is the first node of each component in the tracing node list. "id”: sort nodes based on the nodes' ID. "reverse”: reverse the current node list. |

| [poIVectorCtrl RestorePlotStyle](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectorctrl_restoreplotstyle.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIVectorCtrl_handle RestorePlotStyle style_name` | This command updates the vector controller settings with the new settings specified by the plot style name. | **Inputs:** style_name The name of the plot style. |
| [poIVectorCtrl SetColor](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectorctrl_setcolor.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIVectorCtrl_handle SetColor comp colors` | This command sets the component color. | **Inputs:** comp Possible values are "x", "y", "z", and "resultant". colors "red green blue". |
| [poIVectorCtrl SetColorMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectorctrl_setcolormode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIVectorCtrl_handle SetColorMode name` | This command sets the color mode for vectors. | **Inputs:** name Set to "legend" if vectors should use the legend, or set to "rgb" otherwise. |

| [poIVectorCtrl SetDataType](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectorctrl_setdatatype.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `vector_handle SetDataType data_type_name` | This command sets the data type to be applied for a vector plot (for example, Displacement, Velocity, etc.). If the data type name contains spaces, then the name must be specified inside double quotes (for example, "SPC force"). The data type name must exist in the list of available data types in the results file in order to display a proper vector plot. You can obtain a list of valid data types from the result control by using the GetDataTypeList command. | **Inputs:** data_type_name The name of the data type to be set. |

| [poIVectorCtrl SetEnableState](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectorctrl_setenablestate.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIVectorCtrl_handle SetEnableState state` | This command sets the auto load/unload vector data state. Note: Setting the enable state to false causes the currently loaded data to be unloaded. | **Inputs:** state True if the vector data can be loaded, otherwise false. |

Note: The storage within HyperView can accept a shell layer or a derived quantity of all shell layers (max, min, or extreme). | **Inputs:** layer The shell layer. |

| [poIVectorCtrl SetResultSystem](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectorctrl_setresultsystem.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIVectorCtrl_handle SetResultSystem system_id` | This command sets the result system ID. | **Inputs:** system_id The system ID. |
| [poIVectorCtrl SetScale](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectorctrl_setscale.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIVectorCtrl_handle SetScale scale` | This command sets the scale value used to display vectors. | **Inputs:** scale Set to a float number. |
| [poIVectorCtrl SetScaleMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectorctrl_setscalemode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIVectorCtrl_handle SetScaleMode name` | This command sets the scale mode used to display vectors. | **Inputs:** name magnitude Display vectors by their magnitude. uniform Display vectors using a uniform size. |

Note: The storage within HyperView can accept a shell layer or a derived quantity of all shell layers (max, min, or extreme). | **Inputs:** layer The shell layer label or one of the following derived types: "max" Stores the maximum value of all layers. "min" Stores the minimum value of all layers. "extreme" Stores the maximum absolute value of all layers. |

| [poIVectorCtrl SetThresholdMaxEnableState](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectorctrl_setthresholdmaxenablestate.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIVectorCtrl_handle SetThresholdMaxEnableState` | This command sets the enable/disable state of the maximum threshold. | Not stated |
| [poIVectorCtrl SetThresholdMin](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectorctrl_setthresholdmin.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIVectorCtrl_handle SetThresholdMin` | This command sets the minimum threshold. | Not stated |
| [poIVectorCtrl SetThresholdMinEnableState](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectorctrl_setthresholdminenablestate.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIVectorCtrl_handle SetThresholdMinEnableState` | This command sets the enable/disable state of the minimum threshold. | Not stated |

| [poIVectorDefine SetDataComponent](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectordefine_setdatacomponent.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIVectorDefine_handle SetDataComponent comp` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, sets the data component for the result definition. Returns Success (0) or an error code.Note: The result definition can only be modified if it is not cached. | **Inputs:** comp The data component label (x, y or z): |
| [poIVectorDefine SetDataType](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectordefine_setdatatype.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIVectorDefine_handle SetDataType type` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, sets the data type for the result definition. Returns Success (0) or an error code.Note: The result definition can only be modified if it is not cached. | **Inputs:** type The data type. |
| [poIVectorDefine SetLabel](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectordefine_setlabel.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIVectorDefine_handle SetLabel label` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, sets the label of the result definition. Returns Success (0) or an error code. | **Inputs:** label The result definition label. |
| [poIVectorDefine SetLayer](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectordefine_setlayer.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIVectorDefine_handle SetLayer layer` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, sets the layer of the result definition. Returns Success (0) or an error code.Note: The result definition can only be modified if it is not cached. | **Inputs:** layer The result definition layer. |
| [poIVectorDefine SetLayerFilter](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectordefine_setlayerfilter.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIVectorDefine_handle SetLayerFilter id` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, sets the layer filter ID for this result definition. Returns Success (0) or an error code.Note: The result definition can only be modified if it is not cached. | **Inputs:** id The layer filter ID. |
| [poIVectorDefine SetResultSystem](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectordefine_setresultsystem.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIVectorDefine_handle SetResultSystem sys_id` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, sets the result system ID. This is either a valid system locator of the form "id pool_name" or one of the special identifiers. Returns Success (0) or an error code.Note: The result definition can only be modified if it is not cached. | **Inputs:** sys_id This is either a valid system locator of the form "id pool_name" or one of the special identifiers. 0: global system -1: analysis system -2: elemental system -3: tracking system -4: material system -5: ply system |
| [poIVectorDefine SetShowMidsideNodeResults](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poivectordefine_setshowmidsidenoderesults.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `poIVectorDefine_handle SetShowMidsideNodeResults showMSN` | This command, valid only in Upfront Data Loading Mode, sets the show midside node results state for this result definition.Note: The result definition can only be modified if it not cached. | **Inputs:** showMSN True to show midside node results, false to not show midside node results. |
| [SetEntity](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poibestview_setentity.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `bestview_handle SetEntity type loc_string` | This command sets the entity information for the best view. | **Inputs:** type The type of entity used (element, node, or component). loc_string A string to locate the entity, poolname, and ID. Tip: Combinations can be used (example: {Node 123}). |
| [SetFocusRegionSize](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poibestview_setfocusregionsize.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `bestview_handle SetFocusRegionSize radius` | This command sets the radius of the sphere for all focused view modes (transparency). | **Inputs:** radius The radius in model units. |
| [SetHiddenSet](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poibestview_sethiddenset.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `bestview_handle SetHiddenSet loc_string` | This command sets the set to be used for hiding entities. Any entities in the specified set will be hidden in the view. This will make sure that the given set of entities is not shown for the current best view.Important: This setting is applicable to only following best view modes: global, globallocation, and contour.Other modes of best view will ignore the hidden set settings. | **Inputs:** loc_string A string to locate the set, typically a pool/ID combination (example: {User_Set 1}). |
| [SetNoteDistance](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poibestview_setnotedistance.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `bestview_handle SetNoteDistance distance` | This command sets the note offset distance from the entity center. This distance is not followed during the global, globalregions, globalcomponents, or globalspheres view modes where multiple notes are present and there is a chance of overlap.Note: To change the format and the contents of the best view note, you should create a note inside the current HyperView window and label the note as “master” and then activate a best view. This can also be done using the poINote Class APIs under poIPost.When a master note is present, the format and content of the master note will be followed for all best view evaluations. | **Inputs:** distance The offset in screen percentage (0.1 would be 10% of the screen height and width). |
| [SetNotePosition](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poibestview_setnoteposition.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `bestview_handle SetNotePosition position` | This command sets the position of the note for the best view around the entity.Note: To change the format and the contents of the best view note, you should create a note inside the current HyperView window and label the note as “master” and then activate a best view. This can also be done using the poINote Class APIs under poIPost.When a master note is present, the format and content of the master note will be followed for all best view evaluations. | **Inputs:** position The note position relative to the entity location. Valid options include: aboveleft, aboveright, belowleft, belowright, or auto.Note: The auto option follows the automatic placement of notes around the model as per the ArrangeNotes API described under poIPost. |
| [SetNoteShowSpotID](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poibestview_setnoteshowspotid.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `bestview_handle SetNoteShowSpotID active` | This command sets whether or not the spot ID is shown in the note text. Note: To change the format and the contents of the best view note, you should create a note inside the current HyperView window and label the note as “master” and then activate a best view. This can also be done using the poINote Class APIs under poIPost.When a master note is present, the format and content of the master note will be followed for all best view evaluations. | **Inputs:** active True or false. |
| [SetSimulationIndex](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poibestview_setsimulationindex.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `bestview_handle SetSimulationIndex index` | This command sets the simulation index to be used for the best view. | **Inputs:** index The simulation index to be used. |
| [SetSubcase](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poibestview_setsubcase.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `bestview_handle SetSubcase subcaseid` | This command sets the subcase to be used for the best view. | **Inputs:** subcaseid The subcase ID to be used. |
| [SetViewMode](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poibestview_setviewmode.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `bestview_handle SetViewMode mode` | This command sets the view mode to be used for the best view. | **Inputs:** mode The view mode to be used. Valid options include: global Shows all of the best views simultaneously and the entire model together in isometric view. SetHiddenSet can be used to hide a few components of your choice. globalcomponents Shows all of the best views and their parent components together, with everything else transparent in isometric view. globalregions Shows all of the best views and elements within a region size attached to the best view entities, with everything else transparent in isometric view. globalspheres Shows all of the best views and elements within a sphere around the best view entities, with everything else transparent in isometric view. globalallocation Shows only the best view on the current handle with the entire model visible in isometric view, except the entities inside the hidden set. This is a single best view version of the global view mode. componenttransparency Shows only the best view on the current handle with only its parent component visible in isometric view, everything else in the model in shown in transparency. This is a single best view version of globalcomponents view mode. isolatecomponent Shows only the best view on the current handle with only its parent component visible in a view normal to the current orientation of the element, everything else in the model is hidden. entityzoom Shows only the best view on the current handle with only its parent component visible in a zoomed view normal to the current orientation of the element, everything else in the model is hidden. This is a zoomed in version of isolatecomponent view mode. contour Shows only one best view on the current handle with the entire model visible in a view normal to the current orientation of the element. This is a localized version of the globallocation view mode. transparency Shows only one best view on the current handle with the spherical region around entity visible, in a view normal to the current orientation of the element. This is a single view version of globalspheres view mode. Note: The default view mode is global. |
| [UpdateViewWithCurrent](https://2022.help.altair.com/2022.3/hwdesktop/hwd/topics/reference/tcl/poibestview_updateviewwithcurrent.htm) | HyperView Tcl Modify | `bestview_handle UpdateViewWithCurrent` | This command updates the view with the current view orientation and note locations. When evaluating this view mode for the best view ID, the orientations will be restored instead of resetting. | Not stated |
